   mu_regime_new[1:N] = mu_regime;
   beta10_regime_new[1:N] = beta10_regime;
   tau_regime_new[1:N] = tau_regime;
   beta_usa_regime_new[1:N] = beta_usa_regime;
   beta_eur_regime_new[1:N]= beta_eur_regime;
   gamma_geo_regime_new[1:N] = gamma_geo_regime;  
   prob_forecast[1:N] = probability;
   sigma_s_new[1:N] = sigma_s;
   mean_S_new[1:N] =  mean_S;
   sd_S_new[1:N] =  sd_S; 
   for (t in 1:N){
   	 log_lik[t] =  student_t_lpdf(y[t]| nu,  mean_S[t], sd_S[t]); 
	  /*
     	  In-sample posterior simulated prices
	  */
	  y_sim[t] = student_t_rng(nu , mean_S[t], sd_S[t]);
   }
   /*      
                       One-step-ahead forecast of state probabilities:
                                     p(s_{N+1} | y_{1:N})
						  
        HMM one-step-ahead regime forecast: p(s_{N+1}|y_{1:N}) = A' * p(s_N|y_{1:N})
        Normalize to ensure probabilities sum to 1 and avoid numerical drift.
   */
   prob_forecast[N+1] = to_vector(A' * to_matrix(probability[N]));
   prob_forecast[N+1] /= sum(prob_forecast[N+1]); // normalizes
   /*   
	     ---- LSTM momentum update for t= N+1 ---- 
   */
   v_g_o_new[N+1] = (momentum * v_g_o[N] + epsilon*thetv_o_scalar*n[N]);
   v_n_d_new[N+1] = (momentum * v_n_d[N] + epsilon*thnv_d_scalar*n[N]);
   v_g_i_new[N+1] = (momentum * v_g_i[N] + epsilon*thnv_i_scalar*n[N]);
   v_g_f_new[N+1] = (momentum * v_g_f[N] + epsilon*thnv_f_scalar*n[N]);
   /*
      	 LSTM gate calculations  for forecast at t= N+1
   */
   g_o_new[N+1] = inv_logit(v_g_o_new[N+1] + thnw_o_scalar*h[N] + thnb_o_scalar);
   n_d_new[N+1] = tanh(v_n_d_new[N+1]+ thnw_d_scalar*h[N] + thnb_d_scalar);
   g_i_new[N+1] = inv_logit(v_g_i_new[N+1] + thnw_i_scalar*h[N] + thnb_i_scalar);
   g_f_new[N+1] = inv_logit(v_g_f_new[N+1] + thnw_f_scalar*h[N] + thnb_f_scalar);
   /* 
    	 LSTM cell and hidden state for forecast at t= N+1
   */
   C_new[N+1] =   g_i_new[N+1] *n_d_new[N+1]  + g_f_new[N+1] *C[N]; 
   h_new[N+1] =   g_o_new[N+1] * tanh(C_new[N+1]);
   //
   mu_regime_new[N+1] =  dot_product(mu_1_state_sorted,   prob_forecast[N+1]);
   beta10_regime_new[N+1] = dot_product(beta10_state_sorted,   prob_forecast[N+1]);
   tau_regime_new[N+1] = dot_product(tau_state_sorted,   prob_forecast[N+1]);
   beta_usa_regime_new[N+1] = dot_product(beta_usa_state_sorted,   prob_forecast[N+1]);
   beta_eur_regime_new[N+1] = dot_product(beta_eur_state_sorted,   prob_forecast[N+1]);
   gamma_geo_regime_new[N+1] = dot_product(gamma_geo_state_sorted,   prob_forecast[N+1]);
   //
   n_new[N+1] =  normal_rng(mu_regime_new[N+1] + beta10_regime_new[N+1]*h[N]+ rho*tau_regime_new[N+1]*exp(-0.5*z[N])*log(y[N]/y[N - 1]) , 
                 tau_regime_new[N+1]*sqrt(1-square(rho)));
   /*   
	    ---- Simulating new log-volatility (z_new) at t= N+1 ---- 
	           ( Model 3, pg 64  WANG [2016] )
   */
   z_new[N+1] = n_new[N+1] + phi*(z[N]) + psi*(z[N-1]) +  phi_rv*(rv[N]) 
                + beta_usa_regime_new[N+1]*news_usa_tomorrow +  beta_eur_regime_new[N+1] * news_eur_tomorrow 
			    + gamma_geo_regime_new[N+1] * news_geo_tomorrow;
   /* 
	    ---- Out-of-Sample Projection equations at t= N+1 ----
   */
   // Projects out-of-sample volatility (T+1) from the new latent state "z_new".
   sigma_s_new[N+1] = exp(z_new[N+1]/2);
   
   mu_target_new = EMA_fast[N] + beta * (EMA_fast[N] - EMA_slow[N]);

   deviation_new = y[N] - mu_target_new;
   
   p_chop_new = probability[N, 2]; 
   ou_gate_new = fmin(fmax((p_chop_new - 0.6) / 0.4, 0.0), 1.0);

   sign_deviation_new = deviation_new > 0 ? 1.0 : -1.0;
   ou_reversion_new = - ou_gate_new * phi_reversion * (pow(fabs(deviation_new) + 1e-6, ou_shape) * sign_deviation_new);

   ou_drift_new = ou_reversion_new;
   
   /*   
         ---- Calculate the actual absolute deviation ----
   */
   abs_dev = fabs(deviation_new) + 1e-6;
   /* 
         ---- Calculates the effective reversion rate (theta_eff) ----
		 
     If ou_shape == 1, it reduces to the classic linear 
	 OU formula: theta = ou_gate * phi_reversion
	 
   */
   theta_eff = ou_gate_new * phi_reversion * pow(abs_dev, ou_shape - 1.0);
   /* 
         ---- Calculate the conditional half-life ----
		 
     If the effective rate is practically zero (no reversion force or gate closed),
     we assign a high limit value (e.g., 999.0) to avoid division by zero / infinity.
      
   */
   if (theta_eff > 1e-5) {
       half_life = log(2.0) / theta_eff;
   } else {
       half_life = 999.0; // Indicates the absence of an active reversal in the current state
   }
   
   
   // Prediction of the conditional mean price for T+1 using the 
   // last observed price (y[N]) and its respective jump
   
   mean_S_new[N+1] = y[N] + (mu_mean * y[N] * dt) +  (gamma_grad * liquidity_grad_t[N] * dt) 
                + (gamma_vspri * scalp_index_t[N] * dt)  + (ou_drift_new * dt) + jump[N] * y[N] * dt;
   
   // Calculates the projected standard deviation for T+1, anchored to the 
   // last available raw price and the period's volatility.
   
   sd_S_new[N+1] =   sigma_s_new[N+1]* y[N] * sqrt(dt);
      
   /*  
     	---- Simulating new prices (Posterior) at t= N+1 ---- 
   */
   y_sim[N+1] = student_t_rng(nu , mean_S_new[N+1] , sd_S_new[N+1]); 
   
  // ============================================================================
  // 1. BASE DATA AND DIMENTIONS FOR THE TENT CANDLE (N) AND PREVIOUS ONES
  // ============================================================================

  candle_range_N = high[N] - low[N] + 1e-8;
  body_N         = fabs(y[N] - open[N]);
  body_N1        = fabs(y[N-1] - open[N-1]);
  body_N2        = fabs(y[N-2] - open[N-2]);

  range_N1       = high[N-1] - low[N-1] + 1e-8;
  upper_wick_N   = high[N] - fmax(y[N], open[N]);
  lower_wick_N   = fmin(y[N], open[N]) - low[N];

  // Local Reference Volatility (Standard Deviation)
  local_vol = sd_S_new[N] + 1e-8;   // To avoid look ahead bias by using sigma at t=T

  // ============================================================================
  // 2. WICK METRICS AND NORMALIZED MOMENTUM
  // ============================================================================
  // Note: Ensure you pass High and Low in the Stan 'data' block

  lower_wick_ratio = lower_wick_N / candle_range_N;
  upper_wick_ratio = upper_wick_N / candle_range_N;

  candle_body_norm      = (y[N] - open[N]) / local_vol;
  prev_candle_body_norm = (y[N-1] - open[N-1]) / local_vol;
  pa_reversal_momentum  = candle_body_norm - prev_candle_body_norm;

  // ============================================================================
  // 3. PRICE ACTION STRUCTURES - BULLISH
  // ============================================================================

  // A. Morning Star Pattern
  is_morning_star = (
       (y[N-2] < open[N-2]) &&                           // N-2 is bearish
       (body_N1 / range_N1 < 0.30) &&                    // N-1 is small body/Doji
       (y[N] > open[N]) &&                               // N is bullish
       (y[N] > (open[N-2] + y[N-2]) / 2.0)               // N closes above Nhe midpoint of N-2
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // B. Bullish Engulfing
  is_bullish_engulfing = (
       (y[N-1] < open[N-1]) &&                           // N-1 was bearish
       (y[N] > open[N]) &&                               // N is bullish
       (y[N] >= open[N-1]) &&                            // N close exceeds N-1 open
       (open[N] <= y[N-1])                               // N open is at or below N-1 close
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // C. Hammer (Pinbar Bottom)
  is_hammer = (
       (lower_wick_ratio >= 0.60) &&                     // Lower wick >= 60% of total range
       (upper_wick_ratio <= 0.10) &&                     // Upper wick <= 10%
       (body_N / candle_range_N <= 0.30)                 // Small body <= 30%
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // D. Bullish Break of Structure (BOS)
  max_high_5 = max(high[N-5:N-1]);
  is_bos_bullish = (
       (y[N] > max_high_5) && 
       (y[N-1] <= max_high_5)
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // E. Bullish Liquidity Sweep (Low Rejection)
  min_low_5 = min(low[N-5:N-1]);
  is_liquidity_sweep_bullish = (
       (low[N] < min_low_5) &&                           // Pierced recent swing low
       (y[N] > min_low_5) &&                             // Closed back above support
       (lower_wick_ratio > 0.40)                         // Significant lower wick rejection
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // F. Bullish Order Block
  body_N1_norm = body_N1 / local_vol;
  is_bullish_order_block = (
       (y[N-2] < open[N-2]) &&                           // N-2 was bearish
       (y[N-1] > open[N-1]) &&                           // N-1 bullish displacement
       (body_N1_norm > 1.5) &&                           // Volatility expansion above average
       (low[N] <= high[N-2]) &&                          // Retests top of the Order Block
       (y[N] > low[N-2])                                 // Respects the bottom of the block
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // G. Bullish Fair Value Gap (FVG)
   fvg_bullish_size = fmax(0.0, low[N] - high[N-2]);
   fvg_bullish_norm = fvg_bullish_size / local_vol;

   // H. Piercing Line
   is_piercing_line = (
       (y[N-1] < open[N-1]) &&                           // N-1 is bearish
       (y[N] > open[N]) &&                               // N is bullish
       (open[N] <= low[N-1]) &&                          // Open at or below N-1 low
       (y[N] >= open[N-1] - (body_N1 * 0.50)) &&         // Penetrates > 50% into N-1 body
       (y[N] < open[N-1])                                // Does noN fully engulf
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // I. Selling Climax (Exhaustion)
   body_N_norm = body_N / local_vol;
   is_selling_climax = (
       (y[N] < open[N]) && 
       (body_N_norm > 2.5) && 
       (RSI[N] < 30)
   ) ? 1 : 0;
   
   last_swing_high =  max(high[N-10:N-2]);
   
   is_choch_bullish = (
    y[N] > last_swing_high &&
    y[N-1] <= last_swing_high
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // ============================================================================
   // 4. PRICE ACTION STRUCTURES - BEARISH
   // ============================================================================

   // A. Evening Star Pattern
   is_evening_star = (
     (y[N-2] > open[N-2]) &&                           // N-2 is bullish
     (body_N1 / range_N1 < 0.30) &&                    // N-1 is small body/Doji
     (y[N] < open[N]) &&                               // N is bearish
     (y[N] < (open[N-2] + y[N-2]) / 2.0)               // N closes below Nhe midpoint of N-2
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // B. Shooting Star (Upper Wick RejecNion)
   is_shooting_star = (
     (upper_wick_ratio >= 0.60) &&                     // Upper wick >= 60% of total range
     (lower_wick_ratio <= 0.10) &&                     // Lower wick <= 10%
     (body_N / candle_range_N <= 0.30)                 // Small body <= 30%
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // C. Bearish Change of Character (CHOCH)
   last_swing_low = min(low[N-10:N-2]);
   is_choch_bearish = (
     (y[N] < last_swing_low) && 
     (y[N-1] >= last_swing_low)
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // D. Bearish Breaker Block
   is_bearish_breaker = (
     (y[N-3] > open[N-3]) &&                           // N-3 was bullish (former supporN)
     (y[N-1] < low[N-3])  &&                           // N-1 sNrongly violated N-3 low
     (high[N] >= low[N-3]) &&                          // N retests violated support
     (y[N] <= high[N-3])                               // Respects top of the block
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // E. Bearish Fair Value Gap (FVG)
   fvg_bearish_size = fmax(0.0, low[N-2] - high[N]);
   fvg_bearish_norm = fvg_bearish_size / local_vol;

   // F. Buying Climax (ExhausNion)
   is_buying_climax = (
       (y[N] > open[N]) && 
       (body_N_norm > 2.5) && 
       (RSI[N] > 70)
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // ============================================================================
   // 5. NEUNRAL STRUCTURES / LIQUIDITY CONTEXT
   // ============================================================================

   is_inside_bar = (high[N] <= high[N-1] && low[N] >= low[N-1]) ? 1 : 0;
   is_nr4        = (candle_range_N < min(high[N-3:N-1] - low[N-3:N-1])) ? 1 : 0;

   eq_threshold  = 0.10 * local_vol;
   is_equal_highs = (fabs(high[N-1] - high[N-2]) < eq_threshold) ? 1 : 0;
   is_equal_lows  = (fabs(low[N-1] - low[N-2]) < eq_threshold) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // ============================================================================
   // 6. DOJI & SPINNING TOP VARIATIONS (CANDLE GEOMETRY)
   // ============================================================================
   
   body_ratio_T = body_N / candle_range_N;
   
   // A. Standard Doji (Tiny body ≤ 5% of the total range)
   is_doji = (body_ratio_T <= 0.05) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // B. Dragonfly Doji (Bottom Rejection / Bullish: No upper wick, long lower wick.)
   is_dragonfly_doji = (
     is_doji == 1 && 
     upper_wick_ratio <= 0.05 && 
     lower_wick_ratio >= 0.65
   ) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // C. Gravestone Doji (Top Rejection / Bearish: No lower wick, long upper wick.)
   is_gravestone_doji = (
     is_doji == 1 && 
     lower_wick_ratio <= 0.05 && 
     upper_wick_ratio >= 0.65
   ) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // D. Long-Legged Doji (Extreme indecision with long wicks on both sides.)
   is_long_legged_doji = (
     is_doji == 1 && 
     upper_wick_ratio >= 0.35 && 
     lower_wick_ratio >= 0.35
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // E. Spinning Top (Spinning Top / Indecision: Small body between 5% and 20%)
   is_spinning_top = (body_ratio_T > 0.05 && body_ratio_T <= 0.20) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // Bullish Marubozu
   is_bullish_marubozu = (
     (y[N] > open[N]) && 
     (body_ratio_T >= 0.90) && 
     (upper_wick_ratio <= 0.05) && 
     (lower_wick_ratio <= 0.05) &&
     (body_N_norm > 1.2)
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // Bearish Marubozu
   is_bearish_marubozu = (
     (y[N] < open[N]) && 
     (body_ratio_T >= 0.90) && 
     (upper_wick_ratio <= 0.05) && 
     (lower_wick_ratio <= 0.05) &&
     (body_N_norm > 1.2)
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // ============================================================================
   // 7. ADVANCED SMART MONEY CONCEPTS (SMC) & LIQUIDITY FRAMEWORK
   // ============================================================================

   // 1. Change of Character (CHOCH) - Premature Structural Change
   swing_high_10 = max(high[N-10 : N-2]);
   swing_low_10  = min(low[N-10 : N-2]);

   // 2. Break of Structure (BOS) - Trend Continuation
   swing_high_5 = max(high[N-5 : N-1]);
   swing_low_5  = min(low[N-5 : N-1]);

   // 3. Liquidity Sweeps (Liquidity Sweep at Highs/Lows – False Breakouts)
   is_sweep_highs = (
     high[N] > swing_high_5 && 
     y[N] < swing_high_5 && 
     upper_wick_ratio >= 0.40
   ) ? 1 : 0; // Sales Sweep (Bearish)

   is_sweep_lows = (
     low[N] < swing_low_5 && 
     y[N] > swing_low_5 && 
     lower_wick_ratio >= 0.40
   ) ? 1 : 0; // Buy Sweep (Bullish)

   // 4. Mitigated Order Blocks (OB) at T
   // Bullish OB: Last bearish candle before the strong bullish move that retests support
   is_bullish_ob = (
     y[N-2] < open[N-2] && 
     (y[N-1] - open[N-1]) / local_vol > 1.2 && 
     low[N] <= high[N-2] && 
     y[N] > low[N-2]
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // 5. OB Seller: Last bullish candle before the sharp drop that retests resistance.
   is_bearish_ob = (
     y[N-2] > open[N-2] && 
     (open[N-1] - y[N-1]) / local_vol > 1.2 && 
     high[N] >= low[N-2] && 
     y[N] < high[N-2]
   ) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // ============================================================================
   // 8. DISPLACEMENT
   // ============================================================================

   // Displacement = body significantly larger than the local volatility.

   is_bullish_displacement = (
      y[N] > open[N] &&
      body_N_norm >= 1.20
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   is_bearish_displacement = (
      y[N] < open[N] &&
      body_N_norm >= 1.20
   ) ? 1 : 0;

  // ============================================================================
  // 9. FIBONACCI INPUT VARIABLES CALCULATION
  // ============================================================================

  // Support variables to search for swing high and swing low
  swing_high_val = high[N];
  swing_low_val = low[N];
  lookback_window = 20; // Window size to search for recent swing (e.g., 20 candles)
  
  start_idx = max(1, N - lookback_window + 1);

  // 1. Identify recent Swing High and Swing Low within the lookback window
  for (k in start_idx:N) {
     
     // Find the highest high and lowest low in the recent period
     if (high[k] > swing_high_val) {
        swing_high_val = high[k];
     }
     if (low[k] < swing_low_val) {
        swing_low_val = low[k];
     }
  }

  // Range of the main swing move (Swing Range)
  swing_range = swing_high_val - swing_low_val;
  
  // Initializes the trend vector as neutral.
  for (t in 1:N) {
    trend_dir[t] = 0.0;
  }

  // Calculating the structure requires a minimum history of candles.
  for (t in (2 * M + 1):N) {
    real peak1 = 0.0;
    real peak2 = 0.0;
    real trough1 = 0.0;
    real trough2 = 0.0;

    // 1. Backward scan starting from 't' to locate recent pivots.
    for (i in (t - M):(M + 1)) {
      
      // Evaluates Bullish Pivot (Peak)
      int is_peak = 1;
      for (j in 1:M) {
        if (high[i] <= high[i - j] || high[i] <= high[i + j]) {
          is_peak = 0;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (is_peak == 1) {
        if (peak1 == 0.0) {
          peak1 = high[i];
        } else if (peak2 == 0.0) {
          peak2 = high[i];
        }
      }

      // Evaluates Bearish Pivot (Trough)
      is_trough = 1;
      for (j in 1:M) {
        if (low[i] >= low[i - j] || low[i] >= low[i + j]) {
          is_trough = 0;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (is_trough == 1) {
        if (trough1 == 0.0) {
          trough1 = low[i];
        } else if (trough2 == 0.0) {
          trough2 = low[i];
        }
      }

      // Stops when it finds the two pairs of pivots.
      if (peak1 != 0.0 && peak2 != 0.0 && trough1 != 0.0 && trough2 != 0.0) {
        break;
      }
    }

    //   Conditions of the Moving Average and Price at time 't'
    ma_bullish = (y[t] > EMA_fast[t]) && (EMA_fast[t] > EMA_fast[t - 1]);
    ma_bearish = (y[t] < EMA_fast[t]) && (EMA_fast[t] < EMA_fast[t - 1]);

    //   Pivot Structure Conditions
    pivots_bullish = (farthest_resistance > peak2) && (farthest_support > trough2) && (farthest_resistance != 0.0);
    pivots_bearish = (farthest_resistance < peak2) && (farthest_support < trough2) && (farthest_resistance != 0.0);

    //   Confluent Trend Direction Assignment
    if (pivots_bullish && ma_bullish) {
      trend_dir[t] = 1.0;
    } else if (pivots_bearish && ma_bearish) {
      trend_dir[t] = -1.0;
    } else {
      trend_dir[t] = 0.0;
    }
  }

  if (trend_dir[N] == 1.0) {
     // Uptrend: Retracement levels sit below the high (Discount)
     fib_level_500     = swing_high_val - (0.500 * swing_range);
     fib_level_618     = swing_high_val - (0.618 * swing_range);
     
     // Bullish Target Expansion/Projection above the high
     fib_expansion_1618 = swing_low_val + (1.618 * swing_range);
  } else {
     // Downtrend: Retracement levels sit above the low (Premium)
     fib_level_500     = swing_low_val + (0.500 * swing_range);
     fib_level_618     = swing_low_val + (0.618 * swing_range);
     
     // Bearish Target Expansion/Projection below the low
     fib_expansion_1618 = swing_high_val - (1.618 * swing_range);
  }

  // 3. Price Action Support/Resistance Structure (structure_sr)
  // Defines the nearest structure as the broken high in an uptrend
  // or the broken low in a downtrend.
  structure_sr = (trend_dir[N] == 1.0) ? swing_high_val : swing_low_val; 
   
  // =========================================================================
  // 2. CONFLUENCES OF THE 3 FIBONACCI STRATEGIES
  // =========================================================================
    
    
  // Strategy 1: Confluence between Golden Ratio (61.8%) and S/R Structure
  // Tolerance proportional to local volatility (e.g., 15% of volatility)
  is_fib_sr_confluence = (
     fabs(fib_level_618 - structure_sr) <= (0.15 * local_vol)
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Strategy 2: Discount vs. Premium Zone Filter (50% Level)
  // In an uptrend (trend_dir[N] == 1), looking to buy cheap (price below 50%)
  is_discount_zone_bull = (
     trend_dir[N] == 1.0 && y[N] <= fib_level_500
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // In a downtrend (trend_dir[N] == -1), looking to sell high (price above 50%)
  is_premium_zone_bear = (
     trend_dir[N] == -1.0 && y[N] >= fib_level_500
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Strategy 3: Target / Risk Projection
  // Identifies if current price is within reach of the 161.8% expansion target
  is_fib_target_valid = (
     high[N] < fib_expansion_1618
  ) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // ============================================================================
  // HANGING MAN PATTERN DETECTION
  // ============================================================================

  // Long lower wick (e.g., > 50% of the range)
  has_long_lower_wick = (lower_wick_ratio >= 0.50) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // Small upper wick (e.g., ≤ 15% of the range)
  has_small_upper_wick = (upper_wick_ratio <= 0.15) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // Small body (e.g., body less than or equal to 30% of the total range)
  has_small_body = (body_N / candle_range_N <= 0.30) ? 1 : 0;

  // Context Filter (Must be in Top / Premium zone)
  // Price closes in the upper half of the recent leg
  is_in_premium_zone = (y[N] > fib_level_500) ? 1 : 0;

  if (has_long_lower_wick == 1 && 
      has_small_upper_wick == 1 && 
      has_small_body == 1 && 
      is_in_premium_zone == 1) {
      
      is_hanging_man = 1; // Pattern detected (Warning Signal / Bearish Reversal)
  } else {
      is_hanging_man = 0;
  }
  
  // ===============================================================
  //       Mapping of Confluent Divergences (CVD & RSI)
  // ===============================================================
  
  w = 10;
  
  //  Isolating the reference window (N-11 to N-2)
  price_max_prev = max(y[(N - w - 1):(N - 2)]);
  price_min_prev = min(y[(N - w - 1):(N - 2)]);

  rsi_max_prev   = max(RSI[(N - w - 1):(N - 2)]);
  rsi_min_prev   = min(RSI[(N - w - 1):(N - 2)]);

  cvd_max_prev   = max(CVD[(N - w - 1):(N - 2)]);
  cvd_min_prev   = min(CVD[(N - w - 1):(N - 2)]);

  //   Bearish Divergence: Price hits higher high/equal high, but indicator stays lower
  rsi_bear_recent = ((y[N] >= price_max_prev && RSI[N] < rsi_max_prev) || 
                    (y[N-1] >= price_max_prev && RSI[N-1] < rsi_max_prev)) ? 1 : 0;

  cvd_bear_recent = ((y[N] >= price_max_prev && CVD[N] < cvd_max_prev) || 
                    (y[N-1] >= price_max_prev && CVD[N-1] < cvd_max_prev)) ? 1 : 0;

  //   Bullish Divergence: Price hits lower low/equal low, but indicator stays higher
  rsi_bull_recent = ((y[N] <= price_min_prev && RSI[N] > rsi_min_prev) || 
                    (y[N-1] <= price_min_prev && RSI[N-1] > rsi_min_prev)) ? 1 : 0;

  cvd_bull_recent = ((y[N] <= price_min_prev && CVD[N] > cvd_min_prev) || 
                    (y[N-1] <= price_min_prev && CVD[N-1] > cvd_min_prev)) ? 1 : 0;

  //  Confluence Check
  is_confluent_sell = (rsi_bear_recent == 1 && cvd_bear_recent == 1) ? 1 : 0;
  is_confluent_buy  = (rsi_bull_recent == 1 && cvd_bull_recent == 1) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // ===============================================================
  //    NORMALIZED ELASTICITY (Z-Distance via ATR)
  // ===============================================================
  
  // Distance from the current price to the EMA200, measured in multiples of ATR.
  
  ema_dist_atr = (y[N] - EMA_slow[N]) / (ATR[N] + 1e-8);
  abs_elasticity = fabs(ema_dist_atr);

  // Elasticity Multiplier (Stretch Factor)
  // If the distance is > 2 ATRs, the price is stretched -> Expand the TP
  
  if (abs_elasticity > 2.0) {
      elasticity_mult = 1.35; // +35% expansion in the mean-reversion target
  } else if (abs_elasticity > 1.0) {
      elasticity_mult = 1.15; // +15% expansion
  } else {
      elasticity_mult = 1.00; // Standard distance
  }
  
  big_figure_bull = big_figure_resistence - y[N];
  big_figure_bear = y[N] - big_figure_support;
  buffer_pips = 0.0020; // 20 pips tolerance

  is_news_today = (news_usa[N] == 1 || news_eur[N] == 1 || news_geo[N] == 1) ? 1 : 0;  
  
  // ===========================================
  // LIQUIDITY SWEEP / TURTLE SOUP DETECTION 
  // ===========================================

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  RULES FOR DOUBLE TOP WITH FALSE BREAKOUT (BEARISH SWEEP)
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  // a) The current high breaks the high of the previous pivot (High Liquidity Sweep)
  high_swept_peak = (high[N] > high[N-1]) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // b) The close rejects the breakout and returns inside the previous range
  close_below_peak = (y[N] < high[N-1]) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // c) Presence of a significant upper rejection wick (e.g., ≥ 35% of the candle)
  has_rejection_upper_wick = (upper_wick_ratio >= 0.35) ? 1 : 0;

  // d) Context Filter: Occurs in the Premium Zone
  is_in_premium = (y[N] > fib_level_500) ? 1 : 0;

  if (high_swept_peak == 1 && 
      close_below_peak == 1 && 
      has_rejection_upper_wick == 1 && 
      is_in_premium == 1) {
      
      is_bearish_turtle_soup = 1; // Triggers a sell signal based on liquidity capture.
  } else {
      is_bearish_turtle_soup = 0;
  }
  
  is_bearish_sweep = (
    // Condition A: The high broke through the previous high or the Big Figure.
    (high[N] > swing_high_5 || big_figure_bear <= buffer_pips) &&
    
    // Condition B: The close (y[N]) settled below the swept high.
    y[N] < high[N] &&
    
    // Condition C: The close returned to the high or remained below the BF resistance.
    y[N] <= (swing_high_5 + buffer_pips) &&
    
    // Condition D: Clear rejection shown by the upper wick (>= 40% of the range)
    upper_wick_ratio >= 0.40
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  RULES FOR DOUBLE BOTTOM WITH FALSE BREAKOUT (BULLISH SWEEP)
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  // a) The current low breaks below the low of the previous pivot (Low Liquidity Sweep).
  low_swept_trough = (low[N] < low[N-1]) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // b) The close rejects the low and closes above the previous low.
  close_above_trough = (y[N] > low[N-1]) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // c) Presence of a significant lower rejection wick (e.g., ≥ 35% of the candle)
  has_rejection_lower_wick = (lower_wick_ratio >= 0.35) ? 1 : 0;

  // d) Context Filter: Occurs in a Discount Zone
  is_in_discount = (y[N] <= fib_level_500) ? 1 : 0;

  if (low_swept_trough == 1 && 
      close_above_trough == 1 && 
      has_rejection_lower_wick == 1 && 
      is_in_discount == 1) {
      
      is_bullish_turtle_soup = 1; // Triggers a buy signal via liquidity capture.
  } else {
      is_bullish_turtle_soup = 0;
  }
  
  is_bullish_sweep = (
    // Condition A: The low broke below the previous low or the Big Figure.
    (low[N] < swing_low_5 || big_figure_bull <= buffer_pips) &&
    
    // Condition B: The close (y[N]) held above the swept low.
    y[N] > low[N] &&
    
    // Condition C: The close recovered the swing low or remained above the BF support.
    y[N] >= (swing_low_5 - buffer_pips) &&
    
    // Condition D: Clear rejection shown by the lower wick (>= 40% of the range)
    lower_wick_ratio >= 0.40
  ) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // ============================================================================  
  // 10. Candlestick Microstructural Dynamics Indicators (or Candle Anatomy Metrics)
  // ============================================================================  
  
  
  PVI_N = (y[N] - low[N]) / candle_range_N;
  Lower_Shadow_Ratio = (fmin(open[N], y[N]) - low[N]) / candle_range_N;
  Upper_Shadow_Ratio = (high[N] - fmax(open[N], y[N])) / candle_range_N; 

  // ============================================================================
  // STORMER SETUPS & REGIME SWITCH IDENTIFICATION
  // ============================================================================

  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // A. REGIME SWITCH FILTERS (QUALITY, TREND & STRUCTURE)
  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  has_valid_candle_quality = (
     candle_range_N > 0.0 && (body_N / candle_range_N) >= 0.45
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // 2. Trend Strength Filter: ADX >= 20 to confirm momentum
  has_strong_trend_regime = (ADX[N] >= 20.0) ? 1 : 0;

  // 3. Overall Switch Master State
  switch_active = (
     has_valid_candle_quality == 1 && has_strong_trend_regime == 1
  ) ? 1 : 0;


  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // B. SETUP 1: Reversal Closing Price (RCP)
  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  // Bullish PFR: Candle makes a lower low than previous, but closes above previous close
  raw_pfr_bullish = (
     low[N] < low[N - 1] && y[N] > y[N - 1]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Bearish PFR: Candle makes a higher high than previous, but closes below previous close
  raw_pfr_bearish = (
     high[N] > high[N - 1] && y[N] < y[N - 1]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Filtered PFR Execution Signals via Switch
  is_pfr_bullish = (raw_pfr_bullish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;
  is_pfr_bearish = (raw_pfr_bearish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;


  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // C. SETUP 2: DAVE LANDRY (RETRACEMENT ON EMA20)
  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  // EMA21 slope conditions
  ema20_rising  = (EMA_fast[N] > EMA_fast[N - 1]) ? 1 : 0;
  ema20_falling = (EMA_fast[N] < EMA_fast[N - 1]) ? 1 : 0;

  // Bullish Dave Landry: Upward EMA20 + two consecutive lower lows (correction)
  raw_dave_landry_bullish = (
     ema20_rising == 1 && low[N] < low[N - 1] && low[N - 1] < low[N - 2]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Bearish Dave Landry: Downward EMA20 + two consecutive higher highs (correction)
  raw_dave_landry_bearish = (
     ema20_falling == 1 && high[N] > high[N - 1] && high[N - 1] > high[N - 2]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Filtered Dave Landry Execution Signals via Switch
  is_dave_landry_bullish = (raw_dave_landry_bullish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;
  is_dave_landry_bearish = (raw_dave_landry_bearish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;


  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // D. SETUP 3: PATTERN 123 (PIVOT REVERSAL)
  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  // Bullish 123: Candle N-1 forms the lowest trough (Fundo 2)
  raw_pattern_123_bullish = (
     low[N - 1] < low[N - 2] && low[N] > low[N - 1]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Bearish 123: Candle N-1 forms the highest peak (Topo 2)
  raw_pattern_123_bearish = (
     high[N - 1] > high[N - 2] && high[N] < high[N - 1]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Filtered Pattern 123 Execution Signals via Switch
  is_pattern_123_bullish = (raw_pattern_123_bullish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;
  is_pattern_123_bearish = (raw_pattern_123_bearish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;


  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // E. CONFLUENCE DISPATCHER
  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  // Identifies if PFR and 123 setups trigger simultaneously under the active switch
  is_stormer_confluence_bull = (
     is_pfr_bullish == 1 && is_pattern_123_bullish == 1
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  is_stormer_confluence_bear = (
     is_pfr_bearish == 1 && is_pattern_123_bearish == 1
  ) ? 1 : 0;  


  // ============================================================================
  // 8. AGGREGATED SCORE SYSTEM (PA SCORE)
  // ============================================================================
   
  pa_score_bull = 0.0;
  pa_score_bear = 0.0;

  // --- BULLISH SCORING ---
  if (is_liquidity_sweep_bullish == 1 || is_bullish_turtle_soup == 1) pa_score_bull += 0.25;
  if (is_bullish_order_block == 1)                                    pa_score_bull += 0.20;
  if (fvg_bullish_norm > 0.3)                                         pa_score_bull += 0.15;
  if (is_morning_star == 1 || is_bullish_engulfing == 1)              pa_score_bull += 0.15;
  if (is_hammer == 1 || is_dragonfly_doji == 1)                       pa_score_bull += 0.15;
  if (is_bullish_marubozu == 1 || is_bullish_displacement == 1)       pa_score_bull += 0.10;
  if (is_fib_sr_confluence == 1 && is_discount_zone_bull == 1)        pa_score_bull += 0.20;

  // Fund Reversal / Bottom Absorption
  if (Lower_Shadow_Ratio >= 0.40 && PVI_N >= 0.60 && PVI_N <= 1.00)   pa_score_bull += 0.35;
  
  // --- STORMER SETUPS ADDITIONS (BULLISH) ---
  // Stormer Setups: PFR (0.25), Dave Landry (0.20), Pattern 123 (0.20)
  if (is_pfr_bullish == 1)                                            pa_score_bull += 0.25;
  if (is_dave_landry_bullish == 1)                                    pa_score_bull += 0.20;
  if (is_pattern_123_bullish == 1)                                    pa_score_bull += 0.20;
  
  // High Confluence Bonus: PFR + 123 occurring together with active Switch
  if (is_stormer_confluence_bull == 1)                                pa_score_bull += 0.15;

  // --- BEARISH SCORING ---
  if (is_sweep_highs == 1 || is_bearish_turtle_soup == 1)             pa_score_bear += 0.25;
  if (is_bearish_ob == 1 || is_bearish_breaker == 1)                  pa_score_bear += 0.20;
  if (fvg_bearish_norm > 0.3)                                         pa_score_bear += 0.15;
  if (is_evening_star == 1 || is_shooting_star == 1)                  pa_score_bear += 0.15;
  if (is_gravestone_doji == 1 || is_hanging_man == 1)                 pa_score_bear += 0.15;
  if (is_bearish_marubozu == 1 || is_bearish_displacement == 1)       pa_score_bear += 0.10;
  if (is_fib_sr_confluence == 1 && is_premium_zone_bear == 1)         pa_score_bear += 0.20;

  // Reversal / Top Absorption
  if (Upper_Shadow_Ratio >= 0.40 && PVI_N >= 0.00 && PVI_N <= 0.40)   pa_score_bear += 0.35;
  
  // --- STORMER SETUPS ADDITIONS (BEARISH) ---
  // Stormer Setups: PFR (0.25), Dave Landry (0.20), Pattern 123 (0.20)
  if (is_pfr_bearish == 1)                                            pa_score_bear += 0.25;
  if (is_dave_landry_bearish == 1)                                    pa_score_bear += 0.20;
  if (is_pattern_123_bearish == 1)                                    pa_score_bear += 0.20;
  
  // High Confluence Bonus: PFR + 123 occurring together with active Switch
  if (is_stormer_confluence_bear == 1)                                pa_score_bear += 0.15;

  // =====================
  //   TP/SL multipliers
  // =====================
   
  prob_trend = prob_forecast[N+1][1];  // Calm Regime (Trend)
  prob_scalp = prob_forecast[N+1][2];  // Volatile Regime (Scalp)
    
  k_tp = prob_trend * k_tp_trend + prob_scalp * k_tp_scalp;
  k_sl = prob_trend * k_sl_trend + prob_scalp * k_sl_scalp;
  
  // =============================
  // Quantiles for Stop / Target
  // =============================

  q_std_low  = student_t_quantile(0.05, nu);
  q_std_high = student_t_quantile(0.95, nu);

  // Left tail (risk side)
  q_low  = mean_S_new[N+1] + sd_S_new[N+1] * q_std_low;  // 5% VaR: lower tail quantile (lowest downside risk threshold)  
  
  // Right tail (upside potential)
  q_high = mean_S_new[N+1] + sd_S_new[N+1] * q_std_high;  // 95% quantile: upper tail (extreme upside move)

  ES_up = expected_shortfall_t(mean_S_new[N+1], sd_S_new[N+1], nu, 0.95);   // ES 95% superior
  ES_down = expected_shortfall_t(mean_S_new[N+1], sd_S_new[N+1], nu, 0.05); // ES 5% inferior 
       
  // =====================
  // Directional Metrics 
  // =====================
   
  pa_net_score = (pa_score_bull - pa_score_bear)/2.35;  // Normalized by the total score
  prob_pa_up = inv_logit(pa_net_score);
  
  p_down = student_t_cdf(EMA_fast[N], nu, mean_S_new[N+1] , sd_S_new[N+1]);
  p_up = 1 - p_down;
   
  weight_pa = 0.30;
  p_up_pa = (1 - weight_pa) * p_up + weight_pa * prob_pa_up;
  p_down_pa = 1 - p_up_pa;

  reward_long = q_high - y[N];
  risk_long = y[N] - ES_down;
  rr_long = reward_long /(risk_long + 1e-8);
    
  // ====================-
  
  reward_short = y[N] - q_low;
  risk_short = ES_up - y[N];
  rr_short = reward_short /(risk_short + 1e-8);
      
  lambda_dofp_trend = 0.70;
  lambda_dofp_chop  = 0.25;
   
  lambda_dofp = (prob_trend * lambda_dofp_trend
                  + prob_scalp * lambda_dofp_chop);
				  
  logit_p_up_final = (logit(p_up_pa)+ lambda_dofp * dofp);
   
  p_up_final = inv_logit(logit_p_up_final);
  p_down_final = 1.0 - p_up_final;
   
  ERR_long = p_up_final * reward_long/(p_down_final * risk_long + 1e-8);
  ERR_short = p_down_final * reward_short/(p_up_final * risk_short + 1e-8);
   
  delta_ERR = ERR_long - ERR_short;
  edge = p_up_final - p_down_final;
  
  // ===============================================================
  // SIGNAL DECISION HIERARCHY (Absolute Priority: Reversal)
  // ===============================================================
  
  // LEVEL 1: MAXIMUM DETERMINISTIC TRIGGERS (Sweep / Exhaustion + Displacement)
  // 1.1 HIGH-CONVICTION BULLISH REVERSAL (Bullish Sweep/Exhaustion at BF + Displacement)
  
  if (
      ( (is_bullish_sweep == 1) || (is_confluent_buy == 1 && y[N] > open[N]) ) ||
      (is_news_today == 1 && big_figure_bull <= buffer_pips) || 
      (is_bullish_displacement == 1)
  ) {
      signal = 1;          // Vacuum Buying Force and Institutional Rejection
      p_up_final   = 0.99;
      p_down_final = 0.01;
  }
  
  // 1.2 High-Conviction Sell Reversal (Bearish Sweep/Exaustão at BF + Displacement)
  else if (
      ( (is_bearish_sweep == 1) || (is_confluent_sell == 1 && y[N] < open[N]) ) || 
      (is_news_today == 1 && big_figure_bear <= buffer_pips) || 
      (is_bearish_displacement == 1)
  ) {
      signal = -1;         // Vacuum-Driven Selling Pressure and Institutional Rejection
      p_up_final   = 0.01;
      p_down_final = 0.99;
  }
  // LEVEL 2: INTERMEDIATE STRUCTURAL TRIGGERS (Order Block + Big Figure)  
  // 2.1 BUY ON ORDER BLOCK MITIGATION AT SUPPORT
  else if (is_bullish_ob == 1 && big_figure_bull <= buffer_pips) {
      signal = 1;          // Buy in the OB mitigation zone.
      p_up_final   = 0.85;
      p_down_final = 0.15;
  }
  
  // 2.2 Sell ​​on Order Block mitigation at resistance.
  else if (is_bearish_ob == 1 && big_figure_bear <= buffer_pips) {
      signal = -1;         // Sell ​​in the OB mitigation zone
      p_down_final = 0.85;
      p_up_final   = 0.15;
  }
  // LEVEL 3: MODEL STATISTICAL PROBABILISTIC FALLBACK
  else if(
     edge > 0.05 &&
     ERR_long > 1.00 &&
	 ADX[N] > 25.0 &&     // Filter: Trend strength is building up
     delta_ERR > 0.30 &&	 
	 MACD_Line[N] > 0.00 &&
	 RSI[N] > 50.0 && RSI[N] <= 70.0
   ){
   signal = 1;
   }
   else if(
     edge < -0.05 &&
     ERR_short > 1.00 &&
	 ADX[N] > 25.0 &&
     delta_ERR < -0.30 &&
     MACD_Line[N] < 0.00 &&
     RSI[M] > 30.0 && RSI[N] <= 50.0	      
   ){
   signal = -1;
   }
   else {
    signal = 0;
   }
    
  // ===============================================================
  // 3. (Tail Metrics)
  // ===============================================================
  tail_ratio_up   = (ES_up - mean_S_new[N+1]) / (q_high - mean_S_new[N+1] + 1e-8);
  tail_ratio_down = (mean_S_new[N+1] - ES_down) / (mean_S_new[N+1] - q_low + 1e-8);
  
  tail_ratio_up   = fmin(fmax(tail_ratio_up, 0.5), 2.0);
  tail_ratio_down = fmin(fmax(tail_ratio_down, 0.5), 2.0);

  // ===============================================================
  // 4. Stop/Target with Exhaustion Confluence and Volatility Expansion
  // ===============================================================
  if (signal == 1 && holidays_usa_tomorrow == 0 && holidays_eur_tomorrow == 0) { // BUY

      int is_news_day = (news_usa_tomorrow == 1 || news_eur_tomorrow == 1 || news_geo_tomorrow == 1) ? 1 : 0;
      int is_above_ema200 = (y[N] > EMA_slow[N]) ? 1 : 0;  
      
      // Momentum Deceleration Filter (MACD Histogram turning up)
      int macd_deceleration_buy = (MACD_Histogram[N] > MACD_Histogram[N-1]) ? 1 : 0;
      
      // Exhaustion Gate combining RSI ≤ 30 with Confluent Divergence.
      int is_exhausted = (is_confluent_buy == 1 || (RSI[N] <= 30 && macd_deceleration_buy == 1)) ? 1 : 0;
      
      // Trend Alignment Factor  
      real trend_alignment = is_above_ema200 ? 1.00 : 0.65;
	  
	  // If the price is very extended, give your stop some extra breathing room to avoid getting stopped out by volatility.
	  real sl_buffer = (abs_elasticity > 2.0) ? (0.25 * ATR[N]) : (0.10 * ATR[N]);
           
      // News/Jump Multiplier Scale (news multi)
      real news_mult;
      if (is_news_day == 1) {
          news_mult = is_exhausted ? 0.20 : 0.10;
      } else {
          news_mult = is_exhausted ? 0.15 : 0.08;
      }

      // Dynamic Take Profit (If stretched downwards (Mean Reversion), elasticity extends the TP towards the EMA.)
      tp_price = y[N] + ((q_high - y[N]) * k_tp * tail_ratio_up * news_mult * trend_alignment * elasticity_mult);

      // -----------------------------------------------------------
      // STOP LOSS ESTRUTURAL: Adjusted for Elasticity
      // -----------------------------------------------------------  
      if (is_above_ema200 == 1) {
        sl_price = fmin(farthest_support, EMA_slow[N] - sl_buffer);
      } else {
        sl_price = farthest_support - sl_buffer;
      }

  } else if (signal == -1 && holidays_usa_tomorrow == 0 && holidays_eur_tomorrow == 0) { // SELL
   
      int is_news_day = (news_usa_tomorrow == 1 || news_eur_tomorrow == 1 || news_geo_tomorrow == 1) ? 1 : 0;
      int is_above_ema200 = (y[N] > EMA_slow[N]) ? 1 : 0;
      
      int macd_deceleration_sell = (MACD_Histogram[N] < MACD_Histogram[N-1]) ? 1 : 0;
      
      int is_exhausted = (is_confluent_sell == 1 || (RSI[N] >= 70 && macd_deceleration_sell == 1)) ? 1 : 0;
      
      real trend_alignment = (is_above_ema200 == 0) ? 1.00 : 0.65;
      real sl_buffer = (abs_elasticity > 2.0) ? (0.25 * ATR[N]) : (0.10 * ATR[N]);	  
      
      real news_mult;
      if (is_news_day == 1) {
          news_mult = is_exhausted ? 0.20 : 0.10;
      } else {
          news_mult = is_exhausted ? 0.15 : 0.08;
      }
      
      tp_price = y[N] - ((y[N] - q_low) * k_tp * tail_ratio_down * news_mult * trend_alignment * elasticity_mult);
      
      if (is_above_ema200 == 0) {
        sl_price = fmax(farthest_resistance, EMA_slow[N] + sl_buffer);
      } else {
        sl_price = farthest_resistance + sl_buffer;
      }
   }
   else if (signal == 0  && holidays_usa_tomorrow == 1  ||  holidays_eur_tomorrow == 1) { // NEUTRAL
      tp_price = y[N];
      sl_price = y[N];
   }
}

"""   

GBM_MS_SV_AR2_MomentumLSTM  = pystan.StanModel(model_code=GBM_MS_SV_AR2_MomentumLSTM)

# ==========================================
# Fractal Detector (Local Minima/Maxima)
# ==========================================


def get_fractals(series, window=25):
    """
    Detects local tops and bottoms within a sliding window.
    Increase the 'window' size for fewer arrows (only macro fractals)
    or decrease it to capture micro-oscillations.
    """
    highs = []
    lows = []
    for i in range(window, len(series) - window):
        chunk = series[i - window : i + window + 1]
        if series[i] == np.max(chunk):
            highs.append((i, series[i]))
        elif series[i] == np.min(chunk):
            lows.append((i, series[i]))
    return highs, lows

# Detecting fractal points on the estimated average price
highs, lows = get_fractals(y_hat_mean, window=35)

# ---- Configuration & Setup ----

    nrows=2,

    figsize=(16, 11),
    sharex=True,

)

# Dynamic calculation of the "Dom Forex" zone thickness (2.5% of the estimated price range)
price_range = y_hat_mean.max() - y_hat_mean.min()
zone_thickness = price_range * 0.025

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TOP PANEL: Price Estimate and Forex S/R Zones
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Plot the model's mean estimate and its 95% credible interval
ax1.plot(
    dates,
    y_hat_mean,

    linewidth=2.5,
)

    y_hat_lower,
    y_hat_upper,
    color="#1f77b4",
    alpha=0.10,
    label="95% Credible Interval",
)

# Plotting Bearish (Resistance) Fractals and Zone Projections
if highs:
    high_idx, high_vals = zip(*highs)
    # Red downward triangles adjusted to the EURUSD scale
    ax1.scatter(
        dates[list(high_idx)],
        np.array(high_vals) + (price_range * 0.03),
        marker="v",
        color="#d62728",
        s=80,
        label="Bearish Fractal",
        zorder=5,
    )
    last_res_idx, last_res_val = highs[-1]
    # Soft fill for the active resistance band
    ax1.axhspan(
        last_res_val - zone_thickness,
        last_res_val + zone_thickness,
        color="#d62728",
        alpha=0.08,
        label="Dom Forex Resistance Zone",
    )
    ax1.axhline(
        y=last_res_val, color="#d62728", linestyle="--", alpha=0.6, linewidth=1.5
    )
    # Dynamic text anchoring to prevent clipping on the right edge
    ax1.text(
        dates[-5],
        last_res_val + (zone_thickness * 1.2),
        f"Active Resistance: {last_res_val:.4f}",
        color="#d62728",
        fontsize=10,
        fontweight="bold",
        ha="right",
        va="bottom",
    )
	
	
# Plotting Bullish (Support) Fractals and Zone Projections

    low_idx, low_vals = zip(*lows)
    # Green upward triangles adjusted to the scale
    ax1.scatter(
        dates[list(low_idx)],

    )
    last_sup_idx, last_sup_val = lows[-1]
    # Soft fill for the active support band
    ax1.axhspan(
        last_sup_val - zone_thickness,
        last_sup_val + zone_thickness,
        color="#2ca02c",
        alpha=0.08,
        label="Dom Forex Support Zone",
    )
    ax1.axhline(
        y=last_sup_val, color="#2ca02c", linestyle="--", alpha=0.6, linewidth=1.5
    )
    # Dynamic text anchoring below the support guide line
    ax1.text(
        dates[-5],
        last_sup_val - (zone_thickness * 1.8),
        f"Active Support: {last_sup_val:.4f}",
        color="#2ca02c",
        fontsize=10,
        fontweight="bold",
        ha="right",
        va="top",
    )
	
# --- Axis Framing: Zooming into the core price action (EURUSD Style) ---
# Filters out early variance explosions by framing strictly around the mean path bounds (+-15% padding)

    y_hat_mean.min() - (price_range * 0.15),

#   In Sample regression   #

forecasting_extrapolation_lengh = 1 

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MEAN SQUARED ERROR (MSE) & ROOT MEAN SQUARED ERROR (RMSE)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# RMSE penalizes larger variances quadratically, acting as a key metric for 
# outlier control.                                                                                                                                                         

MSE = mean_squared_error(y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh].values.ravel(),
                         EURUSD[["Close"]].values.ravel())
 
RMSE = math.sqrt(MSE)
print('RMSE: %f' % RMSE)
#  RMSE: 0.011729

# An RMSE of ~ 0.011729 indicates that, on average, the estimated 
# price deviates by a 117 pips from the actual market trajectory.

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MEAN ABSOLUTE ERROR (MAE)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Provides a robust, linear measure of average absolute deviation without 
# over-penalizing spikes. 

mae = mean_absolute_error(y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh].values.ravel(),
                         EURUSD[["Close"]].values.ravel())
print('MAE: %f' % mae)

#  MAE: 0.004414

# An MAE of ~ 0.004414 (44 pips) confirms that the model 
# tracks the central path of the exchange rate.

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# COEFFICIENT OF DETERMINATION (R-SQUARED)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Measures the proportion of total variance in the observed price explained 
# by the model's structural equations. 


coefficient_of_dermination = r2_score(EURUSD[["Close"]].values.ravel(),
                             y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh].values.ravel())
							 
print('R2: %f' % coefficient_of_dermination)
#  R2:  0.937051

# An R2 of 93.70% indicates an 
# ok explanatory power, showing almost perfect alignment over time.

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TWO-SAMPLE KOLMOGOROV-SMIRNOV ADHERENCE TEST
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A non-parametric test comparing the cumulative distribution functions (CDFs) 
# of the observed and estimated series. 

Kolmogorov_Smirnoff_Adherence_One_Dimensional_Sampling_Test = ks_2samp(EURUSD[["Close"]].values.ravel(),
                             y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh].values.ravel())
							 
							 
print('statistic: %f   ,pvalue: %f' % (Kolmogorov_Smirnoff_Adherence_One_Dimensional_Sampling_Test[0],Kolmogorov_Smirnoff_Adherence_One_Dimensional_Sampling_Test[1]))
#   statistic: 0.021033   ,pvalue: 0.999834

#     - The near-zero D-statistic (0.021) indicates negligible divergence.
#
#     - The p-value of 0.999834 confirms that we fail to reject the null hypothesis, 
#       statistically proving that the estimated and observed price distributions 
#       are practically identical.

# ---- QQplot between the two samples ----

# Sort both arrays ( It's needed to align the percentiles in both distributions)
sorted_obs = np.sort(EURUSD[["Close"]].values.ravel().astype(np.float64))
sorted_hat = np.sort(y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh].values.ravel().astype(np.float64))


# Q-Q Plot between y_obs and y_hat

         [sorted_obs.min(), sorted_obs.max()], 'r--')  # 45-degree line

plt.xlabel("Observed Quantiles for EURUSD D1 Close Prices In-Sample model 0")

#########################################
#   Checking for Model 0 residuals      #
#                    homocedasticity    #
#########################################

def calculate_residuals(observed_values, predicted_location, predicted_scale, epsilon=1e-8):
    """
     
    References :

        CRYER, Jonathan D.; CHAN, Kung-Sik. Time Series Analysis: With Applications in R. 2. ed. New York: Springer, 2008.

        HILPISCH, Yves. Python for Finance: Mastering Data-Driven Finance. 2. ed. Sebastopol, CA: O’Reilly Media, 2019.

        HYNDMAN, Rob J.; ATHANASOPOULOS, George. Forecasting: Principles and Practice. 3. ed. Melbourne: OTexts, 2021.
        Disponível em: [https://otexts.com/fpp3/](https://otexts.com/fpp3/). Acesso em: 25 set. 2025.

        TSAY, Ruey S. *Analysis of Financial Time Series . 3. ed. Hoboken, NJ: John Wiley & Sons, 2010.
    
    
    #   Calculates standardized residuals: (Observed - Predicted Location) / Predicted Scale.

    Args:
        observed_values (array-like): The actual observed values
        predicted_location (array-like or scalar): The model's conditional mean (location).
        predicted_scale (array-like): The model's conditional standard deviation (scale/volatility).
        epsilon (float): A small value used to check for near-zero division in the scale.

    Returns:
        numpy.ndarray: The standardized residuals.
    """
    observed_values = np.asarray(observed_values, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    predicted_location = np.asarray(predicted_location, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    predicted_scale = np.asarray(predicted_scale, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    ########*/          \*########
    #
    # 1. Shape/Length Checks
    #
    ########*/          \*########
    if observed_values.shape != predicted_scale.shape:
        raise ValueError("Lengths of observed_values and predicted_scale must match.")
    ########################*/          \*########################
    #
    # Check if location is a scalar or matches the length of the data
    #
    ########################*/          \*########################
    if predicted_location.size > 1 and observed_values.shape != predicted_location.shape:
        raise ValueError("Lengths of observed_values and predicted_location must match if predicted_location is an array.")
    ########################*/          \*########################
    #
    # 2. Safety Check for Zero Division
    #
    ########################*/          \*########################
    if np.any(predicted_scale < epsilon):
        raise ValueError(
            "Predicted scale contains values too close to zero (below {}). Cannot calculate standardized residuals.".format(epsilon)
        )
    ########*/          \*########
    #
    # 3. Calculation
    #
    ########*/          \*########
    return (observed_values - predicted_location) / predicted_scale
	
	
location = conditional_mean_model_00.mean(axis=0)
scale = sd_model_00.mean(axis=0)	

model_0_residuals = calculate_residuals(EURUSD[["Close"]].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
                    location[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh], scale[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh])
					
                
#=============*/        \*=================				   
# Plot Residuals vs. Fitted Values
#=============*/        \*=================

 
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
sns.scatterplot(x=y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh], y=model_0_residuals, alpha=0.7)
plt.axhline(0, color='red', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)
plt.title('Residuals vs. Fitted Values')
plt.xlabel('Fitted Values')
plt.ylabel('Residuals')
plt.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.6)
plt.show()    #  Figure_2.1c_model_0_residuals_vs-fitted_conditional_mean_values

# --- Breusch-Pagan Test ---
# Arguments: residuals, X (exog, independent variables)
# Returns: lagrange_multiplier, p_value_lm, fvalue, p_value_f

X = y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh]
X = sm.add_constant(X)

lm, lm_pvalue, fvalue, f_pvalue = het_breuschpagan(model_0_residuals, X)


print(f"\n--- Breusch-Pagan Test ---")
#  --- Breusch-Pagan Test ---
print(f"Lagrange Multiplier p-value: {lm_pvalue:.4f}")
#  Lagrange Multiplier p-value: 0.7257
print(f"F-statistic p-value:         {f_pvalue:.4f}")
#  F-statistic p-value:          0.7263
if lm_pvalue < 0.05:
    print("  -> Significant p-value suggests presence of heteroscedasticity (reject H0).")
else:
    print("  -> No significant evidence of heteroscedasticity (fail to reject H0).")
	
#  -> No significant evidence of heteroscedasticity (fail to reject H0).

# --- Engle's ARCH-LM Test (Volatility Clustering Check) ---
# Verifies whether any unmodeled ARCH effects remain in the residuals

arch_lm, arch_pvalue, _, _ = het_arch(model_0_residuals, maxlag=5)

print("\n--- Engle's ARCH-LM Test (Volatility Clustering Check) ---")
print(f"ARCH-LM Test p-value: {arch_pvalue:.4f}")

if arch_pvalue < 0.05:
    print("  -> Reject H0: ARCH effects / volatility clusters remain in the residuals.")
else:
    print("  -> Fail to reject H0: The volatility component successfully captured the series dynamics.")

#  -> Fail to reject H0: The volatility component successfully captured the series dynamics.


# =========================================================
# Financial decision making (Bayesian Output Integration)
# =========================================================

# 1. Extracting vectors 
p_buy_dist  = samples["p_up_final"]
p_sell_dist = samples["p_down_final"]
tp_dist     = samples["tp_price"]
sl_dist     = samples["sl_price"]

p_buy  = np.mean(p_buy_dist)
p_sell = np.mean(p_sell_dist)

edge = p_buy - p_sell
threshold = 0.10  # 10% edge dominance requirement

# 2. Isolate MCMC draws where the trade signal was actively triggered
# This prevents neutral draws (signal == 0) from dragging the Stop Loss toward the spot price
if "signal" in samples:
    sig_last = samples["signal"][:, -1] if samples["signal"].ndim > 1 else samples["signal"]
    sell_mask = (sig_last == -1)
    buy_mask  = (sig_last == 1)
else:
    sell_mask = np.ones(len(sl_dist), dtype=bool)
    buy_mask  = np.ones(len(sl_dist), dtype=bool)

# Target calculations considering active MCMC chains only
if edge < -threshold and np.any(sell_mask):
    tp_price = np.mean(tp_dist[sell_mask])
    sl_price = np.mean(sl_dist[sell_mask])
elif edge > threshold and np.any(buy_mask):
    tp_price = np.mean(tp_dist[buy_mask])
    sl_price = np.mean(sl_dist[buy_mask])
else:
    tp_price = np.mean(tp_dist)
    sl_price = np.mean(sl_dist)

# 3. If sl_price still differs due to rounding,
# explicitly anchor it to the resistance level passed to Stan
if edge < -threshold and abs(sl_price - data["farthest_resistance"]) > 1e-4:
    sl_price = data["farthest_resistance"]

# Half-life extraction and Time Stop calculation
half_life_raw = samples["half_life"][:, -1] if ("half_life" in samples and samples["half_life"].ndim > 1) else None

if half_life_raw is not None and np.mean(half_life_raw) < 100.0:
    half_life = np.mean(half_life_raw)
    time_stop_days = min(np.ceil(2.0 * half_life), 15.0)
    time_str = f"Expected Half-Life: {half_life:.1f} bars | Max Holding Period (Time Stop): {time_stop_days:.0f} bars"
else:
    time_str = "Trend Duration: No projected end (Persistent Trend/No Mean Reversion) | Standard Time Stop: 15 bars"

# 4. Formatted output
if edge > threshold:
    print(f"BUY  ({p_buy:.2%}) | Target: {tp_price:.5f} | Stop: {sl_price:.5f} | Edge: {edge:+.2%}")
    print(f"  -> {time_str}")
elif edge < -threshold:
    print(f"SELL ({p_sell:.2%}) | Target: {tp_price:.5f} | Stop: {sl_price:.5f} | Edge: {edge:+.2%}")
    print(f"  -> {time_str}")
else:
    print(f"WAITING: High uncertainty. (Edge: {edge:+.2%})")
	

#  BUY  (99.00%) | Target: 1.14689 | Stop: 1.13764 | Edge: +98.00%
#    -> Trend Duration: No projected end (Persistent Trend/No Mean Reversion) | Standard Time Stop: 15 bars

# ==============================
#  (Model 01) HMC U-NUTs sampler
# ==============================

Quasi_BiGRU_GJR_GARCH_HAR_Deep_UKF_MS = """

/*
                                                                 Abstract
																 
																 
      This model combines an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) with a two-regime Hidden Markov Model (HMM) to model financial prices directly (rather than log-returns). The latent state consists of price and structural
	  momentum (trend), whose dynamics evolve through a nonlinear state-transition function combining regime-dependent momentum persistence, Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) mean reversion, volatility and volatility-of-volatility feedback, 
	  liquidity effects, event-driven jumps, and a hybrid Price Action/Smart Money Concepts (SMC) decision framework to capture complex, time-varying financial dynamics.

      The OU component acts as a smooth, state-dependent restoring force during ranging (choppy) market conditions, allowing the latent trend to revert toward a long-run equilibrium while remaining largely inactive during persistent trending regimes.
	  Market regimes alternate between trending and scalping through the HMM, continuously modulating momentum persistence, liquidity sensitivity, and mean-reversion intensity. A bidirectional GRU injects nonlinear temporal memory into the state transition,
	  enabling the latent dynamics to adapt to complex sequential market patterns beyond classical state-space models. Breakout events combined with EMA compression generate directional jump components, allowing the model to capture abrupt price dislocations associated with market expansions.
	  Observation uncertainty is modeled through a HAR-type volatility equation augmented with GJR-GARCH innovations, macroeconomic news effects, and a multiplicative jump-volatility component, producing a flexible heteroscedastic measurement model.

      Furthermore, we project the latent Student-t predictive distributions into a tiered, rule-governed signal hierarchy. At the primary deterministic level, high-conviction institutional triggers—specifically Liquidity Sweeps, multi-touch Big Figure exhaustion counts, and Order Block mitigations 
	  coupled with strong volume Displacement—override baseline forecasts to enforce sharp directional reversals. In the absence of primary triggers, the model seamlessly falls back on a continuous logit-space decision layer, dynamically weighting directional order-flow proxy metrics (CVD and RSI confluences) 
	  alongside a quantitative Price Action scoring system (encompassing Doji geometries and Fair Value Gaps). Implemented with Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) via Stan, this comprehensive Bayesian framework provides robust inference of latent volatility dynamics and market microstructure signals,
	  significantly enhancing directional risk characterization, signal quality, and financial forecasting.                                            
																  
																  
																  Resumo      
	  
      Este modelo combina um Filtro de Kalman Sem Perfume (UKF) com um Modelo Oculto de Markov (HMM) de dois regimes para modelar preços financeiros diretamente (em vez de retornos logarítmicos). O estado latente é composto pelo preço e pelo momentum estrutural (tendência),
	  cuja dinâmica evolui por meio de uma função de transição de estado não linear que combina persistência de momentum dependente do regime, reversão à média de Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU), feedback de volatilidade e volatilidade da volatilidade, efeitos de liquidez, 
	  saltos desencadeados por eventos e uma estrutura de decisão híbrida de *Price Action* e *Smart Money Concepts* (SMC) para capturar dinâmicas financeiras complexas e variáveis ​​no tempo.

      O componente OU atua como uma força de restauração suave e dependente do estado durante condições de mercado lateralizado (ou volátil/errático), permitindo que a tendência latente reverta para um equilíbrio de longo prazo, enquanto permanece amplamente inativo durante regimes
	  de tendência persistente. Os regimes de mercado alternam entre tendência e *scalping* por meio do HMM, modulando continuamente a persistência do momentum, a sensibilidade à liquidez e a intensidade da reversão à média. Uma GRU bidirecional introduz memória temporal não linear
	  na transição de estado, permitindo que a dinâmica latente se adapte a padrões de mercado sequenciais complexos, indo além dos modelos clássicos de espaço de estados. Eventos de rompimento (*breakout*), combinados com a compressão de EMAs, geram componentes de salto direcional,
	  permitindo que o modelo capture deslocamentos abruptos de preços associados a expansões de mercado. A incerteza da observação é modelada por meio de uma equação de volatilidade do tipo HAR, complementada por inovações GJR-GARCH, efeitos de notícias macroeconômicas e um componente
	  multiplicativo de salto-volatilidade, produzindo um modelo de medição heterocedástico e flexível.

      Além disso, projetamos as distribuições preditivas latentes *Student-t* em uma hierarquia de sinais em níveis, regida por regras. No nível determinístico primário, gatilhos institucionais de alta convicção — especificamente varreduras de liquidez (*Liquidity Sweeps*), contagens de exaustão em níveis de preço redondos 
	  (*Big Figures*) com múltiplos toques e mitigações de *Order Blocks* associadas a um forte deslocamento (*Displacement*) de volume — prevalecem sobre as previsões de base para impor reversões direcionais acentuadas. Na ausência de gatilhos primários, o modelo recorre de forma fluida a uma camada de decisão contínua no espaço *logit*,
	  ponderando dinamicamente métricas *proxy* de fluxo de ordens direcional (confluências de CVD e RSI) juntamente com um sistema quantitativo de pontuação de *Price Action* (abrangendo geometrias *Doji* e *Fair Value Gaps*). Implementada com Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) via Stan, esta estrutura bayesiana abrangente proporciona uma inferência 
	  robusta da dinâmica da volatilidade latente e dos sinais de microestrutura de mercado, aprimorando significativamente a caracterização do risco direcional, a qualidade do sinal e a previsão financeira.

      *  Bibliographic references (ABNT 2 layout) :

         CARPENTER, B. et al. Stan: a probabilistic programming language. Journal of Statistical Software, v. 76, n. 1, 
	     p. 1–32, 2017.
	   
	     CLENOW, A. F. Following the Trend: Diversified managed futures trading. 
	     Chichester: John Wiley & Sons, 2013.	  
	  
         CHUNG, Junyoung et al.Empirical evaluation of gated reTent neural networks on sequence modeling.
         arXiv preprint arXiv:1412.3555, 2014.
         Disponível em: https://arxiv.org/abs/1412.3555

         CHO, Kyunghyun et al.Learning phrase representations using RNN encoder–decoder for statistical machine translation.
         In: CONFERENCE ON EMPIRICAL METHODS IN NATURAL LANGUAGE PROCESSING (EMNLP), 2014, Doha, Qatar.
         Proceedings […]. 2014.DOI: 10.3115/v1/D14-1179

         CORSI, Fulvio.A simple long memory model of realized volatility.Journal of Financial Econometrics, v. 7, n. 2, p. 174–196, 2009.
         DOI: 10.1093/jjfinec/nbp001
	   
	     DAMIANO, L.; PETERSON, B.; WEYLANDT, M. A tutorial on Hidden Markov Models using Stan. 2017. 
	     Disponível em: https://luisdamiano.github.io/stancon18/hmm_stan_tutorial.pdf. Acesso em: 13 jun. 2025.

functions {

  vector predict_ukf(
    vector x,                // Tent state [price, trend]
    matrix P,                // State covariance
    real phi_gru,            // GRU signal weight (influence of learned temporal features)
    real h_gru,              // GRU hidden state (latent nonlinear signal)
    real theta,              // MA(1) coefficient  (impact of previous innovation)
	real phi,                // OU mean-reversion strength (pull toward long-term mean)
    real mu_long,            // Long-term equilibrium level (OU mean)
    real ou_shape,           // Nonlinear scaling of trend reversion (deviation sensitivity)
    real p_chop,             // Probability of chop regime (market being mean-reverting)
    real beta_vol,           // Volatility impact on trend (volatility-driven momentum)
    real innovation_prev_k,  // Previous innovation (obsezation residual)
    real liquidity_grad_t,   // Liquidity gradient (order flow / microstructure pressure)
    real beta_liq,           // Liquidity coefficient  (price sensitivity to liquidity)
    real beta_jump,          // Jump intensity coefficient  (strength of breakout moves)
    real indicator_breakout, // Breakout event  (0 = no breakout, 1 = breakout)
    real scalp_index_t,      // Regime index (0=trend, 1=scalp)
    real ema_signal_t,       // EMA slope (trend strength)
    real ema_compression_t,  // EMA compression (market tension)
    real sigma_observed              // Effective volatility	
  )
{
    // =====================================================
    // STATE DIMENSION
    // =====================================================
    // Two latent states:
    //
    // x[1] = latent price
    // x[2] = trend (structural momentum)
    //

    int L = 2;

    // =====================================================
    // UNSCENTED TRANSFORM PARAMETERS
    // =====================================================
    //
    // alpha controls the spread of the sigma points
    // Typical values: 0.5 – 1
    //
    real alpha  = 0.7;
	
	// lambda defines the transformation weights
    real lambda = alpha * alpha * L - L;
	
	// scaling factor used to generate sigma points
    real gamma  = sqrt(L + lambda);
	
	// =====================================================
    // CHOLESKY DECOMPOSITION OF THE COVARIANCE
    // =====================================================
    //
    // Cholesky decomposition is used to generate the
    // UKF sigma points.
    //
    // A small amount of jitter is added to avoid
    // numerical issues when P is nearly singular.
    //

    matrix[2,2] L_mat =
        cholesky_decompose(P + diag_matrix(rep_vector(1e-9, 2)));
		
    // vector that will store the state's predicted average
    vector[2] x_pred = rep_vector(0.0, 2);
	
	// =====================================================
    // UKF WEIGHTS
    // =====================================================

    // weight of the central sigma point
    real wm0 = lambda / (L + lambda);
	
	// weight of the lateral sigma points
    real wi  = 1.0 / (2.0 * (L + lambda));
	
	// --------------------------------------------------
    // OU gate: It only works in chop mode (smooth, continuous).
	// For sideways market, mean reversion
    // --------------------------------------------------
	
    real ou_gate = fmin(fmax((p_chop - 0.6) / 0.4, 0.0), 1.0);
	
	// --------------------------------------------------
	// Smooth threshold with linear saturation:
    //
	//   p_chop < 0.6 -> OU  off
	//   p_chop > 1.0 -> OU  fully on
    //   Between [0.6,1.0] -> linear transition
	// --------------------------------------------------	
	
	real deviation = x[1] - mu_long;
	// --------------------------------------------------	
	// reversal force:
    //
    // If ou_shape > 1, the reversal is aggressive on large deviations (convex)
    // If ou_shape < 1, the reversal is damped (concave)
    real ou_reversion = - ou_gate * phi * (pow(fabs(deviation) + 1e-6, ou_shape) * (deviation > 0 ? 1 : -1));
	
	
    // =============================
    // NUMERICAL STABILIZATION
    // =============================
	
	// smooth saturation of past innovation
    real innovation_safe = 10 * tanh(innovation_prev_k / 10);
	
	// soft limit for effective volatility
    real sigma_observed_safe = 5 * tanh(sigma_observed / 5);
	
	// volatility term used in the drift
    real vol_term = sigma_observed_safe;

    // =====================================================
    // Regime-Dependent Momentum Persistence
    // =====================================================

    // The persistence of the latent trend depends on the
    // Tent market microstructure regime.

    // We interpolate between two regimes:

    // 1) Trend regime  -> strong momentum persistence
    // 2) Scalping regime -> weak momentum persistence

    // The variable scalp_index_t E [0,1] measures the
    // probability/intensity of a scalping environment.

    // scalp_index_t ~ 0  -> trending market
    // scalp_index_t ~ 1  -> scalping / range-bound market
	
	
	// Persistence of the trend in directional markets
    // When the market is trending, momentum tends to persist.
    real rho_trend = 0.95;

    // Persistence of the trend in scalping environments
    // In scalping regimes momentum decays quickly,
    // producing small oscillations around equilibrium.	
    real rho_scalp = 0.20; 

    // Regime score E (0,1):
    // 0 -> trending regime (persistent momentum)
    // 1 -> scalping regime (mean-reverting / noisy)
    // Combines scalping intensity and EMA trend strength to produce a smooth
    // market-regime score (0 = trending, 1 = scalping) via a logistic transform.
	
    real regime_score =
        inv_logit(0.6 * scalp_index_t - 0.4 * fabs(ema_signal_t));
		
    // Use the regime score to interpolate between high trend persistence
    // and low persistence, allowing the model to adapt momentum dynamics to the market regime.

    real rho_eff =
        rho_trend * (1 - regime_score)
      + rho_scalp * regime_score;
	  
	// Liquidity impact is amplified in scalping regimes,
    // where microstructure effects dominate price formation.

    real beta_liq_eff = beta_liq * regime_score;

    // =====================================================
    // 1️ CENTRAL POINT PROPAGATION
    // =====================================================
    //
    // The first sigma point is the Tent state itself.
    //    
	{
        real price = x[1];
        real trend = x[2];

		
	    // ===== Trend dynamics ----
        
		// Nonlinear AR(1)-like latent momentum process.
        // The persistence (rho_eff) adapts to market regime,
        // while volatility acts as an external driver of momentum magnitude.
        // The tanh() prevents explosive behavior.
		
		// ===== Smooth directional signal  ----
        real direction = tanh(trend);
		
        real trend_new =
              rho_eff * direction     // momentum persistence
            + beta_vol * vol_term;     // impact of volatility
						
		// ==== Jump component (event-driven) ====
		
        // Captures discrete price dislocations triggered by breakout events.
        // EMA compression acts as a "latent pressure" proxy,
        // and the effect is aligned with the Tent trend direction.
		
        real jump_component =
              beta_jump
            * tanh(ema_compression_t)
            * indicator_breakout
            * direction;
			
        // ==== Price dynamics ====
		
        // The price evolves through:
        //
        // 1) structural trend
        // 2) signal learned by the GRU
        // 3) short-term memory of past innovation
        // 4) Half-Life on price decay
		
        real price_new =
              price
            + trend_new
            + phi_gru * h_gru
            + theta   * innovation_safe
            + beta_liq_eff * liquidity_grad_t
			+ ou_reversion            
            + jump_component;
			
			
        // new state vector
		
        vector[2] x0;
        x0[1] = price_new;
        x0[2] = trend_new;

        // update projected average
        x_pred += wm0 * x0;
    }

    // =====================================================
    // 2️ PROPAGATION OF SIGMA POINTS
    // =====================================================
    //
    // Here, we propagate the sigma points generated from
    // the state covariance.
    //
    // This allows capturing the nonlinearities of the dynamics.
    //
    for (i in 1:L){

        // positive and negative sigma points
        vector[2] s_pos = x + gamma * col(L_mat, i);
        vector[2] s_neg = x - gamma * col(L_mat, i);

        // ==== Positive ====
        {
		
            real direction = tanh(s_pos[2]);
						
            real trend_new =
                rho_eff * direction
              + beta_vol * vol_term;
			  			  
            real jump_component =
                  beta_jump
                * tanh(ema_compression_t)
                * indicator_breakout
                * direction;
				
			// For the Positive Sigma Point
            real dev_pos = s_pos[1] - mu_long;
            real ou_rev_pos = - ou_gate * phi * (pow(fabs(dev_pos) + 1e-6, ou_shape) * (dev_pos > 0 ? 1 : -1));	

				
            real price_new =
                s_pos[1]
              + trend_new
              + phi_gru * h_gru
              + theta   * innovation_safe
              + beta_liq_eff * liquidity_grad_t
			  + ou_rev_pos
              + jump_component;
			  			  			    
            vector[2] xp;
            xp[1] = price_new;
            xp[2] = trend_new;

            x_pred += wi * xp;
        }

        // ==== Negative ====
        {
		
            real direction = tanh(s_neg[2]);
						
            real trend_new =
                rho_eff * direction
              + beta_vol * vol_term;
			  			    
            real jump_component =
                  beta_jump
                * tanh(ema_compression_t)
                * indicator_breakout
                * direction;
				
			real dev_neg = s_neg[1] - mu_long;
			real ou_rev_neg = - ou_gate * phi * (pow(fabs(dev_neg) + 1e-6, ou_shape) * (dev_neg > 0 ? 1 : -1));
		
			
            real price_new =
                s_neg[1]
              + trend_new
              + phi_gru * h_gru
              + theta   * innovation_safe
              + beta_liq_eff * liquidity_grad_t
			  + ou_rev_neg
              + jump_component;
			  
			  			  
            vector[2] xn;
            xn[1] = price_new;
            xn[2] = trend_new;

            x_pred += wi * xn;
        }
    }
	// ===================================
    // Final saturation (CRUCIAL & SCALED)
    // ===================================

    // The price of EURUSD has historically never exceeded 1.60 and has never fallen below 0.80.
    // We limit the price state strictly between realistic bands
    x_pred[1] = 1.55 * tanh(x_pred[1] / 1.55); 
	// ==============================
    // ESPECTED STATE 
    // ==============================
    return x_pred;
  }

 real student_t_quantile(real p, real nu) {

    // wide initial limits
    real lower = -50;
    real upper = 50;
    real mid;
    real cdf_mid;

    // tolerance
    real tol = 1e-8;

    // safety
    if (p <= 0) return negative_infinity();
    if (p >= 1) return positive_infinity();

    // bisection
    while (upper - lower > tol) {

      mid = 0.5 * (lower + upper);
      cdf_mid = student_t_cdf(mid, nu, 0, 1);

      if (cdf_mid > p)
        upper = mid;
      else
        lower = mid;
    }

    return 0.5 * (lower + upper);
  }
  
 real expected_shortfall_t(
    real mu,
    real sigma,
    real nu,
    real alpha   // ex: 0.95 ou 0.05
  ) {
    // Calculation of Expected Shortfall (ES) for Dynamic Stop Loss
    // It reflects the average losses in the tail, making it more reliable than simple VaR.
		
    // standardized t-quantile
    real q = student_t_quantile(alpha, nu);

    // quantile density
    real pdf_q = exp(student_t_lpdf(q | nu, 0, 1));

    // Standardized ES
    real es_std = (nu + square(q)) / (nu - 1)  * pdf_q;

    // Scaled ES 
    if (alpha < 0.5)
        return mu - sigma * es_std / alpha;
    else
        return mu + sigma * es_std / (1 - alpha);
    }
		
  real ReLU(real x) {
   return fmax(x,1e-12);
  }
  /*
