   // ============================================================================
   // DOJI & SPINNING TOP VARIATIONS (CANDLE GEOMETRY)
   // ============================================================================
   
   body_ratio_T = body_N / candle_range_N;
   
   // A. Standard Doji (Tiny body ≤ 5% of the total range)
   is_doji = (body_ratio_T <= 0.05) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // B. Dragonfly Doji (Bottom Rejection / Bullish: No upper wick, long lower wick.)
   is_dragonfly_doji = (
     is_doji == 1 && 
     upper_wick_ratio <= 0.05 && 
     lower_wick_ratio >= 0.65
   ) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // C. Gravestone Doji (Top Rejection / Bearish: No lower wick, long upper wick.)
   is_gravestone_doji = (
     is_doji == 1 && 
     lower_wick_ratio <= 0.05 && 
     upper_wick_ratio >= 0.65
   ) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // D. Long-Legged Doji (Extreme indecision with long wicks on both sides.)
   is_long_legged_doji = (
     is_doji == 1 && 
     upper_wick_ratio >= 0.35 && 
     lower_wick_ratio >= 0.35
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // E. Spinning Top (Spinning Top / Indecision: Small body between 5% and 20%)
   is_spinning_top = (body_ratio_T > 0.05 && body_ratio_T <= 0.20) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // Bullish Marubozu
   is_bullish_marubozu = (
     (y[T] > open[T]) && 
     (body_ratio_T >= 0.90) && 
     (upper_wick_ratio <= 0.05) && 
     (lower_wick_ratio <= 0.05) &&
     (body_N_norm > 1.2)
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // Bearish Marubozu
   is_bearish_marubozu = (
     (y[T] < open[T]) && 
     (body_ratio_T >= 0.90) && 
     (upper_wick_ratio <= 0.05) && 
     (lower_wick_ratio <= 0.05) &&
     (body_N_norm > 1.2)
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // ============================================================================
   // ADVANCED SMART MONEY CONCEPTS (SMC) & LIQUIDITY FRAMEWORK
   // ============================================================================

   // 1. Change of Character (CHOCH) - Premature Structural Change
   swing_high_10 = max(high[T-10 : T-2]);
   swing_low_10  = min(low[T-10 : T-2]);

   // 2. Break of Structure (BOS) - Trend Continuation
   swing_high_5 = max(high[T-5 : T-1]);
   swing_low_5  = min(low[T-5 : T-1]);

   // 3. Liquidity Sweeps (Liquidity Sweep at Highs/Lows – False Breakouts)
   is_sweep_highs = (
     high[T] > swing_high_5 && 
     y[T] < swing_high_5 && 
     upper_wick_ratio >= 0.40
   ) ? 1 : 0; // Sales Sweep (Bearish)

   is_sweep_lows = (
     low[T] < swing_low_5 && 
     y[T] > swing_low_5 && 
     lower_wick_ratio >= 0.40
   ) ? 1 : 0; // Buy Sweep (Bullish)

   // 4. Mitigated Order Blocks (OB) at T
   // Bullish OB: Last bearish candle before the strong bullish move that retests support
   is_bullish_ob = (
     y[T-2] < open[T-2] && 
     (y[T-1] - open[T-1]) / local_vol > 1.2 && 
     low[T] <= high[T-2] && 
     y[T] > low[T-2]
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   // OB Seller: Last bullish candle before the sharp drop that retests resistance.
   is_bearish_ob = (
     y[T-2] > open[T-2] && 
     (open[T-1] - y[T-1]) / local_vol > 1.2 && 
     high[T] >= low[T-2] && 
     y[T] < high[T-2]
   ) ? 1 : 0;
   
   // ============================================================================
   // 6. DISPLACEMENT
   // ============================================================================

   // Displacement = body significantly larger than the local volatility.

   is_bullish_displacement = (
      y[T] > open[T] &&
      body_N_norm >= 1.20
   ) ? 1 : 0;

   is_bearish_displacement = (
      y[T] < open[T] &&
      body_N_norm >= 1.20
   ) ? 1 : 0;
   
  // ============================================================================
  // 7. FIBONACCI INPUT VARIABLES CALCULATION
  // ============================================================================

  // Support variables to search for swing high and swing low
  swing_high_val = high[T];
  swing_low_val = low[T];
  lookback_window = 20; // Window size to search for recent swing (e.g., 20 candles)
  
  start_idx = max(1, T - lookback_window + 1);

  // 1. Identify recent Swing High and Swing Low within the lookback window
  for (k in start_idx:T) {
     
     // Find the highest high and lowest low in the recent period
     if (high[k] > swing_high_val) {
        swing_high_val = high[k];
     }
     if (low[k] < swing_low_val) {
        swing_low_val = low[k];
     }
  }

  // Range of the main swing move (Swing Range)
  swing_range = swing_high_val - swing_low_val;
  
  // Initializes the trend vector as neutral.
  for (t in 1:T) {
    trend_dir[t] = 0.0;
  }

  // Calculating the structure requires a minimum history of candles.
  for (t in (2 * M + 1):T) {
    real peak1 = 0.0;
    real peak2 = 0.0;
    real trough1 = 0.0;
    real trough2 = 0.0;

    // 1. Backward scan starting from 't' to locate recent pivots.
    for (i in (t - M):(M + 1)) {
      
      // Evaluates Bullish Pivot (Peak)
      int is_peak = 1;
      for (j in 1:M) {
        if (high[i] <= high[i - j] || high[i] <= high[i + j]) {
          is_peak = 0;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (is_peak == 1) {
        if (peak1 == 0.0) {
          peak1 = high[i];
        } else if (peak2 == 0.0) {
          peak2 = high[i];
        }
      }

      // Evaluates Bearish Pivot (Trough)
      is_trough = 1;
      for (j in 1:M) {
        if (low[i] >= low[i - j] || low[i] >= low[i + j]) {
          is_trough = 0;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (is_trough == 1) {
        if (trough1 == 0.0) {
          trough1 = low[i];
        } else if (trough2 == 0.0) {
          trough2 = low[i];
        }
      }

      // Interrompe quando encontrar os dois pares de pivôs
      if (peak1 != 0.0 && peak2 != 0.0 && trough1 != 0.0 && trough2 != 0.0) {
        break;
      }
    }

    //   Conditions of the Moving Average and Price at time 't'
    ma_bullish = (y[t] > ema_fast[t]) && (ema_fast[t] > ema_fast[t - 1]);
    ma_bearish = (y[t] < ema_fast[t]) && (ema_fast[t] < ema_fast[t - 1]);

    //   Pivot Structure Conditions
    pivots_bullish = (farthest_resistance > peak2) && (farthest_support > trough2) && (farthest_resistance != 0.0);
    pivots_bearish = (farthest_resistance < peak2) && (farthest_support < trough2) && (farthest_resistance != 0.0);

    //   Confluent Trend Direction Assignment
    if (pivots_bullish && ma_bullish) {
      trend_dir[t] = 1.0;
    } else if (pivots_bearish && ma_bearish) {
      trend_dir[t] = -1.0;
    } else {
      trend_dir[t] = 0.0;
    }
  }

  if (trend_dir[T] == 1.0) {
     // Uptrend: Retracement levels sit below the high (Discount)
     fib_level_500     = swing_high_val - (0.500 * swing_range);
     fib_level_618     = swing_high_val - (0.618 * swing_range);
     
     // Bullish Target Expansion/Projection above the high
     fib_expansion_1618 = swing_low_val + (1.618 * swing_range);
  } else {
     // Downtrend: Retracement levels sit above the low (Premium)
     fib_level_500     = swing_low_val + (0.500 * swing_range);
     fib_level_618     = swing_low_val + (0.618 * swing_range);
     
     // Bearish Target Expansion/Projection below the low
     fib_expansion_1618 = swing_high_val - (1.618 * swing_range);
  }

  // 3. Price Action Support/Resistance Structure (structure_sr)
  // Defines the nearest structure as the broken high in an uptrend
  // or the broken low in a downtrend.
  structure_sr = (trend_dir[T] == 1.0) ? swing_high_val : swing_low_val;   
   
  // =========================================================================
  //   CONFLUENCES OF THE 3 FIBONACCI STRATEGIES
  // =========================================================================
    
    
  // Strategy 1: Confluence between Golden Ratio (61.8%) and S/R Structure
  // Tolerance proportional to local volatility (e.g., 15% of volatility)
  is_fib_sr_confluence = (
     fabs(fib_level_618 - structure_sr) <= (0.15 * local_vol)
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Strategy 2: Discount vs. Premium Zone Filter (50% Level)
  // In an uptrend (trend_dir[T] == 1), looking to buy cheap (price below 50%)
  is_discount_zone_bull = (
     trend_dir[T] == 1.0 && y[T] <= fib_level_500
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // In a downtrend (trend_dir[T] == -1), looking to sell high (price above 50%)
  is_premium_zone_bear = (
     trend_dir[T] == -1.0 && y[T] >= fib_level_500
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Strategy 3: Target / Risk Projection
  // Identifies if current price is within reach of the 161.8% expansion target
  is_fib_target_valid = (
     high[T] < fib_expansion_1618
  ) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // ============================================================================
  // HANGING MAN PATTERN DETECTION
  // ============================================================================

  // Long lower wick (e.g., > 50% of the range)
  has_long_lower_wick = (lower_wick_ratio >= 0.50) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // Small upper wick (e.g., ≤ 15% of the range)
  has_small_upper_wick = (upper_wick_ratio <= 0.15) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // Small body (e.g., body less than or equal to 30% of the total range)
  has_small_body = (body_N / candle_range_N <= 0.30) ? 1 : 0;

  // Context Filter (Must be in Top / Premium zone)
  // Price closes in the upper half of the recent leg
  is_in_premium_zone = (y[T] > fib_level_500) ? 1 : 0;

  if (has_long_lower_wick == 1 && 
      has_small_upper_wick == 1 && 
      has_small_body == 1 && 
      is_in_premium_zone == 1) {
      
      is_hanging_man = 1; // Pattern detected (Warning Signal / Bearish Reversal)
  } else {
      is_hanging_man = 0;
  }
  
  // ===============================================================
  //       Mapping of Confluent Divergences (CVD & RSI)
  // ===============================================================
  
  window = 10;
  
  //  Isolating the reference window (T-11 to T-2)
  price_max_prev = max(y[(T - window - 1):(T - 2)]);
  price_min_prev = min(y[(T - window - 1):(T - 2)]);

  rsi_max_prev   = max(RSI[(T - window - 1):(T - 2)]);
  rsi_min_prev   = min(RSI[(T - window - 1):(T - 2)]);

  cvd_max_prev   = max(CVD[(T - window - 1):(T - 2)]);
  cvd_min_prev   = min(CVD[(T - window - 1):(T - 2)]);

  //   Bearish Divergence: Price hits higher high/equal high, but indicator stays lower
  rsi_bear_recent = ((y[T] >= price_max_prev && RSI[T] < rsi_max_prev) || 
                    (y[T-1] >= price_max_prev && RSI[T-1] < rsi_max_prev)) ? 1 : 0;

  cvd_bear_recent = ((y[T] >= price_max_prev && CVD[T] < cvd_max_prev) || 
                    (y[T-1] >= price_max_prev && CVD[T-1] < cvd_max_prev)) ? 1 : 0;

  //   Bullish Divergence: Price hits lower low/equal low, but indicator stays higher
  rsi_bull_recent = ((y[T] <= price_min_prev && RSI[T] > rsi_min_prev) || 
                    (y[T-1] <= price_min_prev && RSI[T-1] > rsi_min_prev)) ? 1 : 0;

  cvd_bull_recent = ((y[T] <= price_min_prev && CVD[T] > cvd_min_prev) || 
                    (y[T-1] <= price_min_prev && CVD[T-1] > cvd_min_prev)) ? 1 : 0;

  //  Confluence Check
  is_confluent_sell = (rsi_bear_recent == 1 && cvd_bear_recent == 1) ? 1 : 0;
  is_confluent_buy  = (rsi_bull_recent == 1 && cvd_bull_recent == 1) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // ===============================================================
  //    NORMALIZED ELASTICITY (Z-Distance via ATR)
  // ===============================================================
  
  // Distance from the current price to the EMA200, measured in multiples of ATR.
  
  ema_dist_atr = (y[T] - ema_slow[T]) / (ATR[T] + 1e-8);
  abs_elasticity = fabs(ema_dist_atr);

  // Elasticity Multiplier (Stretch Factor)
  // If the distance is > 2 ATRs, the price is stretched -> Expand the TP
  
  if (abs_elasticity > 2.0) {
      elasticity_mult = 1.35; // +35% expansion in the mean-reversion target
  } else if (abs_elasticity > 1.0) {
      elasticity_mult = 1.15; // +15% expansion
  } else {
      elasticity_mult = 1.00; // Standard distance
  }
  
  big_figure_bull = big_figure_resistence - y[T];
  big_figure_bear = y[T] - big_figure_support;
  buffer_pips = 0.0020; // 20 pips tolerance

  is_news_today = (news_usa[T] == 1 || news_eur[T] == 1 || news_geo[T] == 1) ? 1 : 0;  
  
  
  // ===========================================
  // LIQUIDITY SWEEP / TURTLE SOUP DETECTION 
  // ===========================================

  //  RULES FOR DOUBLE TOP WITH FALSE BREAKOUT (BEARISH SWEEP)
  
  // a) The current high breaks the high of the previous pivot (High Liquidity Sweep)
  high_swept_peak = (high[T] > high[T-1]) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // b) The close rejects the breakout and returns inside the previous range
  close_below_peak = (y[T] < high[T-1]) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // c) Presence of a significant upper rejection wick (e.g., ≥ 35% of the candle)
  has_rejection_upper_wick = (upper_wick_ratio >= 0.35) ? 1 : 0;

  // d) Context Filter: Occurs in the Premium Zone
  is_in_premium = (y[T] > fib_level_500) ? 1 : 0;

  if (high_swept_peak == 1 && 
      close_below_peak == 1 && 
      has_rejection_upper_wick == 1 && 
      is_in_premium == 1) {
      
      is_bearish_turtle_soup = 1; // Triggers a sell signal based on liquidity capture.
  } else {
      is_bearish_turtle_soup = 0;
  }
  
  is_bearish_sweep = (
    // Condition A: The high broke through the previous high or the Big Figure.
    (high[T] > swing_high_5 || big_figure_bear <= buffer_pips) &&
    
    // Condition B: The close (y[T]) settled below the swept high.
    y[T] < high[T] &&
    
    // Condition C: The close returned to the high or remained below the BF resistance.
    y[T] <= (swing_high_5 + buffer_pips) &&
    
    // Condition D: Clear rejection shown by the upper wick (>= 40% of the range)
    upper_wick_ratio >= 0.40
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  //  RULES FOR DOUBLE BOTTOM WITH FALSE BREAKOUT (BULLISH SWEEP)
  
  // a) The current low breaks below the low of the previous pivot (Low Liquidity Sweep).
  low_swept_trough = (low[T] < low[T-1]) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // b) The close rejects the low and closes above the previous low.
  close_above_trough = (y[T] > low[T-1]) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // c) Presence of a significant lower rejection wick (e.g., ≥ 35% of the candle)
  has_rejection_lower_wick = (lower_wick_ratio >= 0.35) ? 1 : 0;

  // d) Context Filter: Occurs in a Discount Zone
  is_in_discount = (y[T] <= fib_level_500) ? 1 : 0;

  if (low_swept_trough == 1 && 
      close_above_trough == 1 && 
      has_rejection_lower_wick == 1 && 
      is_in_discount == 1) {
      
      is_bullish_turtle_soup = 1; // Triggers a buy signal via liquidity capture.
  } else {
      is_bullish_turtle_soup = 0;
  }
  
  is_bullish_sweep = (
    // Condition A: The low broke below the previous low or the Big Figure.
    (low[T] < swing_low_5 || big_figure_bull <= buffer_pips) &&
    
    // Condition B: The close (y[T]) held above the swept low.
    y[T] > low[T] &&
    
    // Condition C: The close recovered the swing low or remained above the BF support.
    y[T] >= (swing_low_5 - buffer_pips) &&
    
    // Condition D: Clear rejection shown by the lower wick (>= 40% of the range)
    lower_wick_ratio >= 0.40
  ) ? 1 : 0;
  
  // ============================================================================  
  // 7. Candlestick Microstructural Dynamics Indicators (or Candle Anatomy Metrics)
  // ============================================================================  
    
  PVI_T = (y[T] - low[T]) / candle_range_N;
  Lower_Shadow_Ratio = (fmin(open[T], y[T]) - low[T]) / candle_range_N;
  Upper_Shadow_Ratio = (high[T] - fmax(open[T], y[T])) / candle_range_N; 
  
  // ============================================================================
  // STORMER SETUPS & REGIME SWITCH IDENTIFICATION
  // ============================================================================

  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // A. REGIME SWITCH FILTERS (QUALITY, TREND & STRUCTURE)
  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  has_valid_candle_quality = (
     candle_range_N > 0.0 && (body_N / candle_range_N) >= 0.45
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // 2. Trend Strength Filter: ADX >= 20 to confirm momentum
  has_strong_trend_regime = (ADX[T] >= 20.0) ? 1 : 0;

  // 3. Overall Switch Master State
  switch_active = (
     has_valid_candle_quality == 1 && has_strong_trend_regime == 1
  ) ? 1 : 0;


  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // B. SETUP 1: Reversal Closing Price (RCP)
  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  // Bullish PFR: Candle makes a lower low than previous, but closes above previous close
  raw_pfr_bullish = (
     low[T] < low[T - 1] && y[T] > y[T - 1]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Bearish PFR: Candle makes a higher high than previous, but closes below previous close
  raw_pfr_bearish = (
     high[T] > high[T - 1] && y[T] < y[T - 1]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Filtered PFR Execution Signals via Switch
  is_pfr_bullish = (raw_pfr_bullish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;
  is_pfr_bearish = (raw_pfr_bearish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;


  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // C. SETUP 2: DAVE LANDRY (RETRACEMENT ON EMA20)
  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  // EMA21 slope conditions
  ema20_rising  = (ema_fast[T] > ema_fast[T - 1]) ? 1 : 0;
  ema20_falling = (ema_fast[T] < ema_fast[T - 1]) ? 1 : 0;

  // Bullish Dave Landry: Upward EMA20 + two consecutive lower lows (correction)
  raw_dave_landry_bullish = (
     ema20_rising == 1 && low[T] < low[T - 1] && low[T - 1] < low[T - 2]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Bearish Dave Landry: Downward EMA20 + two consecutive higher highs (correction)
  raw_dave_landry_bearish = (
     ema20_falling == 1 && high[T] > high[T - 1] && high[T - 1] > high[T - 2]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Filtered Dave Landry Execution Signals via Switch
  is_dave_landry_bullish = (raw_dave_landry_bullish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;
  is_dave_landry_bearish = (raw_dave_landry_bearish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;


  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // D. SETUP 3: PATTERN 123 (PIVOT REVERSAL)
  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  // Bullish 123: Candle T-1 forms the lowest trough (Fundo 2)
  raw_pattern_123_bullish = (
     low[T - 1] < low[T - 2] && low[T] > low[T - 1]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Bearish 123: Candle T-1 forms the highest peak (Topo 2)
  raw_pattern_123_bearish = (
     high[T - 1] > high[T - 2] && high[T] < high[T - 1]
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  // Filtered Pattern 123 Execution Signals via Switch
  is_pattern_123_bullish = (raw_pattern_123_bullish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;
  is_pattern_123_bearish = (raw_pattern_123_bearish == 1 && switch_active == 1) ? 1 : 0;


  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // E. CONFLUENCE DISPATCHER
  // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  
  // Identifies if PFR and 123 setups trigger simultaneously under the active switch
  is_stormer_confluence_bull = (
     is_pfr_bullish == 1 && is_pattern_123_bullish == 1
  ) ? 1 : 0;

  is_stormer_confluence_bear = (
     is_pfr_bearish == 1 && is_pattern_123_bearish == 1
  ) ? 1 : 0;  


  // ============================================================================
  // 8. AGGREGATED SCORE SYSTEM (PA SCORE)
  // ============================================================================
   
  pa_score_bull = 0.0;
  pa_score_bear = 0.0;

  // --- BULLISH SCORING ---
  if (is_liquidity_sweep_bullish == 1 || is_bullish_turtle_soup == 1) pa_score_bull += 0.25;
  if (is_bullish_order_block == 1)                                    pa_score_bull += 0.20;
  if (fvg_bullish_norm > 0.3)                                         pa_score_bull += 0.15;
  if (is_morning_star == 1 || is_bullish_engulfing == 1)              pa_score_bull += 0.15;
  if (is_hammer == 1 || is_dragonfly_doji == 1)                       pa_score_bull += 0.15;
  if (is_bullish_marubozu == 1 || is_bullish_displacement == 1)       pa_score_bull += 0.10;
  if (is_fib_sr_confluence == 1 && is_discount_zone_bull == 1)        pa_score_bull += 0.20;

  // Fund Reversal / Bottom Absorption
  if (Lower_Shadow_Ratio >= 0.40 && PVI_T >= 0.60 && PVI_T <= 1.00)   pa_score_bull += 0.35;
  
  // --- STORMER SETUPS ADDITIONS (BULLISH) ---
  // Stormer Setups: PFR (0.25), Dave Landry (0.20), Pattern 123 (0.20)
  if (is_pfr_bullish == 1)                                            pa_score_bull += 0.25;
  if (is_dave_landry_bullish == 1)                                    pa_score_bull += 0.20;
  if (is_pattern_123_bullish == 1)                                    pa_score_bull += 0.20;
  
  // High Confluence Bonus: PFR + 123 occurring together with active Switch
  if (is_stormer_confluence_bull == 1)                                pa_score_bull += 0.15;

  // --- BEARISH SCORING ---
  if (is_sweep_highs == 1 || is_bearish_turtle_soup == 1)             pa_score_bear += 0.25;
  if (is_bearish_ob == 1 || is_bearish_breaker == 1)                  pa_score_bear += 0.20;
  if (fvg_bearish_norm > 0.3)                                         pa_score_bear += 0.15;
  if (is_evening_star == 1 || is_shooting_star == 1)                  pa_score_bear += 0.15;
  if (is_gravestone_doji == 1 || is_hanging_man == 1)                 pa_score_bear += 0.15;
  if (is_bearish_marubozu == 1 || is_bearish_displacement == 1)       pa_score_bear += 0.10;
  if (is_fib_sr_confluence == 1 && is_premium_zone_bear == 1)         pa_score_bear += 0.20;

  // Reversal / Top Absorption
  if (Upper_Shadow_Ratio >= 0.40 && PVI_T >= 0.00 && PVI_T <= 0.40)   pa_score_bear += 0.35;
  
  // --- STORMER SETUPS ADDITIONS (BEARISH) ---
  // Stormer Setups: PFR (0.25), Dave Landry (0.20), Pattern 123 (0.20)
  if (is_pfr_bearish == 1)                                            pa_score_bear += 0.25;
  if (is_dave_landry_bearish == 1)                                    pa_score_bear += 0.20;
  if (is_pattern_123_bearish == 1)                                    pa_score_bear += 0.20;
  
  // High Confluence Bonus: PFR + 123 occurring together with active Switch
  if (is_stormer_confluence_bear == 1)                                pa_score_bear += 0.15;

  
   // ========-- Out of sample at T+1 ========--
   y_sim[T+1] = student_t_rng(nu, mu[T+1], sigma[T+1]);
  
   // =====================
   // Directional Metrics 
   // =====================
  
   p_down = student_t_cdf(ema_fast[T], nu, mu[T+1] , sigma[T+1]);
   p_up = 1 - p_down;
  
   pa_net_score = (pa_score_bull - pa_score_bear)/2.35;
   prob_pa_up = inv_logit(pa_net_score);
  
   weight_pa = 0.30;
   p_up_pa = (1 - weight_pa) * p_up + weight_pa * prob_pa_up;
   p_down_pa = 1 - p_up_pa;

   reward_long = q_high - y[T];
   risk_long = y[T] - ES_down;
   rr_long = reward_long /(risk_long + 1e-8);
    
   // ====================-
  
   reward_short = y[T] - q_low;
   risk_short = ES_up - y[T];
   rr_short = reward_short /(risk_short + 1e-8);
     
   // Signaling logic 
    
   lambda_dofp_trend = 0.70;
   lambda_dofp_chop  = 0.25;
   
   lambda_dofp = (prob_trend * lambda_dofp_trend
                  + prob_scalp * lambda_dofp_chop);
				  
   logit_p_up_final = (logit(p_up_pa)+ lambda_dofp * dofp);
   
   p_up_final = inv_logit(logit_p_up_final);
   p_down_final = 1.0 - p_up_final;
  
   ERR_long = p_up_final * reward_long/(p_down_final * risk_long + 1e-8);
   ERR_short = p_down_final * reward_short/(p_up_final * risk_short + 1e-8);	
   
   delta_ERR = ERR_long - ERR_short;
   edge = p_up_final - p_down_final; 
  
   // =====================
   // Tail metrics
   // =====================
  
   tail_ratio_up   = (ES_up - mu[T+1]) / (q_high - mu[T+1] + 1e-8);
   tail_ratio_down = (mu[T+1] - ES_down) / (mu[T+1] - q_low + 1e-8);
  
   tail_ratio_up = fmin(fmax(tail_ratio_up, 0.5), 2.0);
   tail_ratio_down = fmin(fmax(tail_ratio_down, 0.5), 2.0);
  
   // ===============================================================
   // SIGNAL DECISION HIERARCHY (Absolute Priority: Reversal)
   // ===============================================================
 
   // LEVEL 1: MAXIMUM DETERMINISTIC TRIGGERS (Sweep / Exhaustion + Displacement)
   // 1.1 HIGH-CONVICTION BULLISH REVERSAL (Bullish Sweep/Exhaustion at BF + Displacement)
  
   if (
      ( (is_bullish_sweep == 1) || (is_confluent_buy == 1 && y[T] > open[T]) ) ||
      (is_news_today == 1 && big_figure_bull <= buffer_pips) || 
      (is_bullish_displacement == 1)
   ) {
      signal = 1;          // Vacuum Buying Force and Institutional Rejection
      p_up_final   = 0.99;
      p_down_final = 0.01;
  }
  
  // 1.2 High-Conviction Sell Reversal (Bearish Sweep/Exhaustion at the BF + Displacement)
  else if (
      ( (is_bearish_sweep == 1) || (is_confluent_sell == 1 && y[T] < open[T]) ) || 
      (is_news_today == 1 && big_figure_bear <= buffer_pips) || 
      (is_bearish_displacement == 1)
  ) {
      signal = -1;         // Vacuum-Driven Selling Pressure and Institutional Rejection
      p_up_final   = 0.01;
      p_down_final = 0.99;
  }
  // LEVEL 2: INTERMEDIATE STRUCTURAL TRIGGERS (Order Block + Big Figure)  
  // 2.1 BUY ON ORDER BLOCK MITIGATION AT SUPPORT
  else if (is_bullish_ob == 1 && big_figure_bull <= buffer_pips) {
      signal = 1;          // Compra na zona de mitigação do OB
      p_up_final   = 0.85;
      p_down_final = 0.15;
  }
  
  // 2.2 Sell ​​on Order Block mitigation at resistance.
  else if (is_bearish_ob == 1 && big_figure_bear <= buffer_pips) {
      signal = -1;         // Venda na zona de mitigação do OB
      p_down_final = 0.85;
      p_up_final   = 0.15;
  }
  // NÍVEL 3: MODEL STATISTICAL PROBABILISTIC FALLBACK 

  else if(
     edge > 0.05 &&
     ERR_long > 1.00 &&
	 ADX[T] > 25.0 &&     // Filter: Trend strength is building up
     delta_ERR > 0.30 &&	 
	 MACD_Line[T] > 0.00 &&
	 RSI[T] > 50.0 && RSI[T] < 70.0
   ){
   signal = 1;
   }
   else if(
     edge < -0.05 &&
     ERR_short > 1.00 &&
	 ADX[T] > 25.0 &&
     delta_ERR < -0.30 &&
     MACD_Line[T] < 0.00 &&
     RSI[T] > 30.0 && RSI[T] < 50.0	      
   ){
   signal = -1;
   }
   else {
    signal = 0;
   }
    
  // ===============================================================
  // 4. Stop / Target com Confluência Exaustiva & Expansão de Volatilidade
  // ===============================================================
  if (signal == 1 && holidays_usa_tomorrow == 0 && holidays_eur_tomorrow == 0) { // BUY

      int is_news_day = (news_usa_tomorrow == 1 || news_eur_tomorrow == 1 || news_geo_tomorrow == 1) ? 1 : 0;
      int is_above_ema200 = (y[T] > ema_slow[T]) ? 1 : 0;  
      
      // Momentum Deceleration Filter (MACD Histogram turning up)
      int macd_deceleration_buy = (MACD_Histogram[T] > MACD_Histogram[T-1]) ? 1 : 0;
      
      // Exhaustion Gate combining RSI ≤ 30 with Confluent Divergence.
      int is_exhausted = (is_confluent_buy == 1 || (RSI[T] <= 30 && macd_deceleration_buy == 1)) ? 1 : 0;
      
      // Trend Alignment Factor  
      real trend_alignment = is_above_ema200 ? 1.00 : 0.65;
	  
	  // If the price is very extended, give your stop some extra breathing room to avoid getting stopped out by volatility.
	  real sl_buffer = (abs_elasticity > 2.0) ? (0.25 * ATR[T]) : (0.10 * ATR[T]);
           
      // News/Jump Multiplier Scale (news multi)
      real news_mult;
      if (is_news_day == 1) {
          news_mult = is_exhausted ? 0.15 : 0.08;
      } else {
          news_mult = is_exhausted ? 0.06 : 0.04;
      }

      // Dynamic Take Profit (If stretched downwards (Mean Reversion), elasticity extends the TP towards the EMA.)
      tp_price = y[T] + ((q_high - y[T]) * k_tp * tail_ratio_up * news_mult * trend_alignment * elasticity_mult);

      // -----------------------------------------------------------
      // STOP LOSS ESTRUTURAL: Adjusted for Elasticity
      // -----------------------------------------------------------  
      if (is_above_ema200 == 1) {
        sl_price = fmin(farthest_support, ema_slow[T] - sl_buffer);
      } else {
        sl_price = farthest_support - sl_buffer;
      }

  } else if (signal == -1 && holidays_usa_tomorrow == 0 && holidays_eur_tomorrow == 0) { // SELL
   
      int is_news_day = (news_usa_tomorrow == 1 || news_eur_tomorrow == 1 || news_geo_tomorrow == 1) ? 1 : 0;
      int is_above_ema200 = (y[T] > ema_slow[T]) ? 1 : 0;
      
      int macd_deceleration_sell = (MACD_Histogram[T] < MACD_Histogram[T-1]) ? 1 : 0;
      
      int is_exhausted = (is_confluent_sell == 1 || (RSI[T] >= 70 && macd_deceleration_sell == 1)) ? 1 : 0;
      
      real trend_alignment = (is_above_ema200 == 0) ? 1.00 : 0.65;
      real sl_buffer = (abs_elasticity > 2.0) ? (0.25 * ATR[T]) : (0.10 * ATR[T]);	  
      
      real news_mult;
      if (is_news_day == 1) {
          news_mult = is_exhausted ? 0.15 : 0.08;
      } else {
          news_mult = is_exhausted ? 0.06 : 0.04;
      }
      
      tp_price = y[T] - ((y[T] - q_low) * k_tp * tail_ratio_down * news_mult * trend_alignment * elasticity_mult);
      
      if (is_above_ema200 == 0) {
        sl_price = fmax(farthest_resistance, ema_slow[T] + sl_buffer);
      } else {
        sl_price = farthest_resistance + sl_buffer;
      }
   }
   else if (signal == 0 || (holidays_usa_tomorrow == 1  ||  holidays_eur_tomorrow == 1)) { // NEUTRAL
      tp_price = y[T];
      sl_price = y[T];
   }
}

"""
Quasi_BiGRU_GJR_GARCH_HAR_Deep_UKF_MS = pystan.StanModel(model_code = Quasi_BiGRU_GJR_GARCH_HAR_Deep_UKF_MS)

data  = ({'T': len(EURUSD[["Close"]].values.flatten().astype(np.float64)),
          'y' : EURUSD[["Close"]].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  # Half-Wave Calculation - leg exhaustion / reversal signal == (reversal_lag - 1)/2
		  'M' : np.int(np.round((4-1)/2)),                    #  Accordingly with Figure_1.1f ( EURSUD_Close_fractional_difference ACF) 
		  'open': EURUSD[["Open"]].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  'high': EURUSD[["High"]].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  'close' : EURUSD[["Close"]].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  'low':  EURUSD[["Low"]].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  'rv': relativity_volatility.values.flatten().astype(np.float64)*100,
		  'date': dates_dummies.values.astype(np.int64),
		  'news_usa' :  EURUSD[["news_usa"]].values.flatten().astype(np.int64),
		  'news_eur' :  EURUSD[["news_eur"]].values.flatten().astype(np.int64),
		  'news_geo' :  EURUSD[["news_geo"]].values.flatten().astype(np.int64),
		  'news_usa_tomorrow' : news_next.loc[0,'news_usa'],
		  'news_eur_tomorrow' : news_next.loc[0,'news_eur'],
		  'news_geo_tomorrow' : news_next.loc[0,'news_geo'],
		  'holidays_usa_tomorrow' : news_next.loc[0,'holiday_usa'],
		  'holidays_eur_tomorrow' : news_next.loc[0,'holiday_eur'],
          'RSI' : features["rsi"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
          'MACD_Line' : features["MACD_Line"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
          'MACD_Histogram' : features["MACD_Histogram"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  "ADX": features["ADX"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  "ATR": features["ATR"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  "CVD": features["CVD"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  "dofp": np.mean(features["dofp"][-10:].values.flatten().astype(np.float64)),   # last 10 days mean 
		  'D_breakout' : features["D_breakout"].values.flatten().astype(np.int64),
          'D_near_sr'  : features["D_near_sr"].values.flatten().astype(np.int64),
          'D_ema_pressure' : features["D_ema_pressure"].values.flatten().astype(np.int64),
		  'liquidity_grad_t' : liquidity_grad_t.values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  'ema_medium' : features["ema_medium"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  'ema_fast' : features["ema_fast"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
          'ema_slow'  : features["ema_slow"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64), 		  
		  'scalp_index_t' : scalp_index_smoothed.values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  'ema_signal_t' :  features["ema_signal"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  'ema_compression_t' : features["ema_compression"].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
		  'farthest_resistance': 1.1488,           
		  'farthest_support': 1.1389, 
          'big_figure_resistence' : 1.14793,
          'big_figure_support' :  1.14030,   
		  'nu': 2.13029,                        # Accordingly with  Figure1.1d ( EURSUD_Close_fractional_difference pdf),
          'tau_slab':      sigma_slab,          # Empirical standard deviation (SD) of the exceedances/jumps
          'p_jump':        jump_probability,    # Daily exceedance rate / Jump probability (λ)
          'tau_spike':     sigma_spike,         # Standard deviation (SD) of continuous baseline noise (without jumps)
          'scale_slab':    scale_gpd,           # Scale parameter (σ_gpd) of the GPD fitted to the excesses
		  'J' : 30,	# Abritary choice , but literature recommends something around 30 lags.(To capture long range clusters)	  
		 })
		 		 		 		 
control = {}
control['max_treedepth'] = 3
control['adapt_delta'] = 0.9999

# ==========================================
# Fractal Detector (Local Minima/Maxima)
# ==========================================


def get_fractals(series, window=25):
    """
    Detects local tops and bottoms within a sliding window.
    Increase the 'window' size for fewer arrows (only macro fractals)
    or decrease it to capture micro-oscillations.
    """
    highs = []
    lows = []
    for i in range(window, len(series) - window):
        chunk = series[i - window : i + window + 1]
        if series[i] == np.max(chunk):
            highs.append((i, series[i]))
        elif series[i] == np.min(chunk):
            lows.append((i, series[i]))
    return highs, lows

# Detecting fractal points on the estimated average price
highs, lows = get_fractals(y_hat_mean, window=35)

# ===== Configuration & Setup ----

############################
#   In Sample regression   #
#        metrics KPI       #
#                          #
############################   

forecasting_extrapolation_lengh = 1 

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MEAN SQUARED ERROR (MSE) & ROOT MEAN SQUARED ERROR (RMSE)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# RMSE penalizes larger variances quadratically, acting as a key metric for 
# outlier control.                                                                                                                                                         

MSE = mean_squared_error(y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh].values.ravel(),
                         EURUSD[["Close"]].values.ravel())
 
RMSE = math.sqrt(MSE)
print('RMSE: %f' % RMSE)
#  RMSE: 0.000783

# An RMSE of ~0.000783 indicates that, on average, the estimated 
# price deviates by  78 pips from the actual market trajectory.

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MEAN ABSOLUTE ERROR (MAE)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Provides a robust, linear measure of average absolute deviation without 
# over-penalizing spikes. 

mae = mean_absolute_error(y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh].values.ravel(),
                         EURUSD[["Close"]].values.ravel())
print('MAE: %f' % mae)

#  MAE: 0.000428

# An MAE of ~ 0.000428 (43 pips) confirms that the model 
# tracks the central path of the exchange rate with exceptionally high fidelity.


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# COEFFICIENT OF DETERMINATION (R-SQUARED)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Measures the proportion of total variance in the observed price explained 
# by the model's structural equations. 


coefficient_of_dermination = r2_score(EURUSD[["Close"]].values.ravel(),
                             y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh].values.ravel())
							 
print('R2: %f' % coefficient_of_dermination)
#  R2:  0.999720

# An R2 of 99.97% indicates an 
# outstanding explanatory power, showing almost perfect alignment over time.

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TWO-SAMPLE KOLMOGOROV-SMIRNOV ADHERENCE TEST
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A non-parametric test comparing the cumulative distribution functions (CDFs) 
# of the observed and estimated series. 

Kolmogorov_Smirnoff_Adherence_One_Dimensional_Sampling_Test = ks_2samp(EURUSD[["Close"]].values.ravel(),
                             y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh].values.ravel())
							 
							 
print('statistic: %f   ,pvalue: %f' % (Kolmogorov_Smirnoff_Adherence_One_Dimensional_Sampling_Test[0],Kolmogorov_Smirnoff_Adherence_One_Dimensional_Sampling_Test[1]))
#   statistic: 0.013384   ,pvalue: 1.000000

#     - The near-zero D-statistic (0.013) indicates negligible divergence.
#
#     - The p-value of 1.000000 confirms that we fail to reject the null hypothesis, 
#       statistically proving that the estimated and observed price distributions 
#       are practically identical.


# ---- QQplot between the two samples ----

# Sort both arrays ( It's needed to align the percentiles in both distributions)
sorted_obs = np.sort(EURUSD[["Close"]].values.ravel().astype(np.float64))
sorted_hat = np.sort(y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh].values.ravel().astype(np.float64))


# Q-Q Plot between y_obs and y_hat
plt.figure(figsize=(6, 6))
plt.plot(sorted_obs, sorted_hat, 'o')
plt.plot([sorted_obs.min(), sorted_obs.max()],
         [sorted_obs.min(), sorted_obs.max()], 'r--')  # 45-degree line
plt.title("Q-Q Plot: y_hat vs y_obs model 0")
plt.xlabel("Observed Quantiles for EURUSD D1 Close Prices In-Sample model 0")
plt.ylabel("Predicted Quantiles for EURUSD D1 Close Prices In-Sample model 0")

#       The Q-Q plot shows good adherence along the median and upper tail of the distribution.

#########################################
#   Checking for Model 0 residuals      #
#                    homocedasticity    #
#########################################

def calculate_residuals(observed_values, predicted_location, predicted_scale, epsilon=1e-8):
    """
     
    References :

        CRYER, Jonathan D.; CHAN, Kung-Sik. Time Series Analysis: With Applications in R. 2. ed. New York: Springer, 2008.

        HILPISCH, Yves. Python for Finance: Mastering Data-Driven Finance. 2. ed. Sebastopol, CA: O’Reilly Media, 2019.

        HYNDMAN, Rob J.; ATHANASOPOULOS, George. Forecasting: Principles and Practice. 3. ed. Melbourne: OTexts, 2021.
        Disponível em: [https://otexts.com/fpp3/](https://otexts.com/fpp3/). Acesso em: 25 set. 2025.

        TSAY, Ruey S. *Analysis of Financial Time Series . 3. ed. Hoboken, NJ: John Wiley & Sons, 2010.
    
    
    #   Calculates standardized residuals: (Observed - Predicted Location) / Predicted Scale.

    Args:
        observed_values (array-like): The actual observed values
        predicted_location (array-like or scalar): The model's conditional mean (location).
        predicted_scale (array-like): The model's conditional standard deviation (scale/volatility).
        epsilon (float): A small value used to check for near-zero division in the scale.

    Returns:
        numpy.ndarray: The standardized residuals.
    """
    observed_values = np.asarray(observed_values, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    predicted_location = np.asarray(predicted_location, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    predicted_scale = np.asarray(predicted_scale, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    ########*/          \*########
    #
    # 1. Shape/Length Checks
    #
    ########*/          \*########
    if observed_values.shape != predicted_scale.shape:
        raise ValueError("Lengths of observed_values and predicted_scale must match.")
    ########################*/          \*########################
    #
    # Check if location is a scalar or matches the length of the data
    #
    ########################*/          \*########################
    if predicted_location.size > 1 and observed_values.shape != predicted_location.shape:
        raise ValueError("Lengths of observed_values and predicted_location must match if predicted_location is an array.")
    ########################*/          \*########################
    #
    # 2. Safety Check for Zero Division
    #
    ########################*/          \*########################
    if np.any(predicted_scale < epsilon):
        raise ValueError(
            "Predicted scale contains values too close to zero (below {}). Cannot calculate standardized residuals.".format(epsilon)
        )
    ########*/          \*########
    #
    # 3. Calculation
    #
    ########*/          \*########
    return (observed_values - predicted_location) / predicted_scale
	
	
location = conditional_mean_model_01.mean(axis=0)
scale = sd_model_01.mean(axis=0)	

model_01_residuals = calculate_residuals(EURUSD[["Close"]].values.flatten().astype(np.float64),
                    location[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh], scale[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh])
					
                
#=============*/        \*=================				   
#    Plot Residuals vs. Fitted Values
#=============*/        \*=================
 
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
sns.scatterplot(x=y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh], y=model_01_residuals, alpha=0.7)
plt.axhline(0, color='red', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)
plt.title('Residuals vs. Fitted Values')
plt.xlabel('Fitted Values')
plt.ylabel('Residuals')
plt.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.6)
plt.show()    #  Figure_2.2c_model_0_residuals_vs-fitted_conditional_mean_values

# --- Breusch-Pagan Test ---
# Arguments: residuals, X (exog, independent variables)
# Returns: lagrange_multiplier, p_value_lm, fvalue, p_value_f

X = y_hat_mean[:-forecasting_extrapolation_lengh]
X = sm.add_constant(X)

lm, lm_pvalue, fvalue, f_pvalue = het_breuschpagan(model_01_residuals, X)


print(f"\n--- Breusch-Pagan Test ---")
#  --- Breusch-Pagan Test ---
print(f"Lagrange Multiplier p-value: {lm_pvalue:.4f}")
#  Lagrange Multiplier p-value: 0.0000
print(f"F-statistic p-value:         {f_pvalue:.4f}")
#  F-statistic p-value:         0.0000
if lm_pvalue < 0.05:
    print("  -> Significant p-value suggests presence of heteroscedasticity (reject H0).")
else:
    print("  -> No significant evidence of heteroscedasticity (fail to reject H0).")

#   -> Significant p-value suggests presence of heteroscedasticity (reject H0).


# --- Engle's ARCH-LM Test (Volatility Clustering Check) ---
# Verifies whether any unmodeled ARCH effects remain in the residuals

arch_lm, arch_pvalue, _, _ = het_arch(model_01_residuals, maxlag=5)

print("\n--- Engle's ARCH-LM Test (Volatility Clustering Check) ---")
print(f"ARCH-LM Test p-value: {arch_pvalue:.4f}")

if arch_pvalue < 0.05:
    print("  -> Reject H0: ARCH effects / volatility clusters remain in the residuals.")
else:
    print("  -> Fail to reject H0: The volatility compocurrent successfully captured the series dynamics.")

#    -> Reject H0: ARCH effects / volatility clusters remain in the residuals.


# =========================================================
# Financial decision making (Bayesian Output Integration)
# =========================================================

# 1. Extracting vectors 
p_buy_dist  = samples["p_up_final"]
p_sell_dist = samples["p_down_final"]
tp_dist     = samples["tp_price"]
sl_dist     = samples["sl_price"]

p_buy  = np.mean(p_buy_dist)
p_sell = np.mean(p_sell_dist)

edge = p_buy - p_sell
threshold = 0.10  # 10% edge dominance requirement

# 2. Isolate MCMC draws where the trade signal was actively triggered
# This prevents neutral draws (signal == 0) from dragging the Stop Loss toward the spot price
if "signal" in samples:
    sig_last = samples["signal"][:, -1] if samples["signal"].ndim > 1 else samples["signal"]
    sell_mask = (sig_last == -1)
    buy_mask  = (sig_last == 1)
else:
    sell_mask = np.ones(len(sl_dist), dtype=bool)
    buy_mask  = np.ones(len(sl_dist), dtype=bool)

# Target calculations considering active MCMC chains only
if edge < -threshold and np.any(sell_mask):
    tp_price = np.mean(tp_dist[sell_mask])
    sl_price = np.mean(sl_dist[sell_mask])
elif edge > threshold and np.any(buy_mask):
    tp_price = np.mean(tp_dist[buy_mask])
    sl_price = np.mean(sl_dist[buy_mask])
else:
    tp_price = np.mean(tp_dist)
    sl_price = np.mean(sl_dist)

# 3. If sl_price still differs due to rounding,
# explicitly anchor it to the resistance level passed to Stan
if edge < -threshold and abs(sl_price - data["farthest_resistance"]) > 1e-4:
    sl_price = data["farthest_resistance"]

# Half-life extraction and Time Stop calculation
half_life_raw = samples["half_life"][:, -1] if ("half_life" in samples and samples["half_life"].ndim > 1) else None

if half_life_raw is not None and np.mean(half_life_raw) < 100.0:
    half_life = np.mean(half_life_raw)
    time_stop_days = min(np.ceil(2.0 * half_life), 15.0)
    time_str = f"Expected Half-Life: {half_life:.1f} bars | Max Holding Period (Time Stop): {time_stop_days:.0f} bars"
else:
    time_str = "Trend Duration: No projected end (Persistent Trend/No Mean Reversion) | Standard Time Stop: 15 bars"

# 4. Formatted output
if edge > threshold:
    print(f"BUY  ({p_buy:.2%}) | Target: {tp_price:.5f} | Stop: {sl_price:.5f} | Edge: {edge:+.2%}")
    print(f"  -> {time_str}")
elif edge < -threshold:
    print(f"SELL ({p_sell:.2%}) | Target: {tp_price:.5f} | Stop: {sl_price:.5f} | Edge: {edge:+.2%}")
    print(f"  -> {time_str}")
else:
    print(f"WAITING: High uncertainty. (Edge: {edge:+.2%})")
	
# BUY  (99.00%) | Target: 1.14796 | Stop: 1.13764 | Edge: +98.00%
#    -> Trend Duration: No projected end (Persistent Trend/No Mean Reversion) | Standard Time Stop: 15 bars
  
################*/           *\ ################
# 
#               ==== Passage 3 ====
#       Decision and final considerations .
#
################*/           *\ ################

#            Estimate       SE
#  elpd_waic  1856.96    31.53
#  p_waic       12.43        -

# Model 01 Score :

#            Estimate         SE
#  elpd_waic 402650.24  11361.39
#  p_waic     85568.03        -


#    1856.96 (elpd_waic)  : 
#
#        *   The value elpd_waic = 1856.96 represents the expected log predictive density 
#		     of the model for new data, based on posterior simulations.More positive (less negative) values 
#		     indicate better out-of-sample predictive performance. In absolute terms, the value itself has no specific threshold,
#		     it is only meaningful when compared to other models fitted to the same data.
#
#            A model with higher elpd_waic (closer to zero) is generally preferred, assuming differences exceed the combined standard
#		     error of comparison.
#
#	 31.53 (SE - Standard Error)  : 
#
#
#         *  This represents the uncertainty in the elpd_waic estimate.
#            A smaller standard error indicates a more precise estimate. When comparing models,
#	         if the difference in elpd_waic values is larger than the sum of their standard errors,
#	         it suggests a statistically significant difference in predictive performance.
#
#               Model 00: elpd_waic = 1856.96, SE = 31.53
#
#               Model 01: elpd_waic = 402650.24, SE = 11361.39
#               
#               Difference = -400793.28,
#               Sum of SEs = 31.53 + 11361.39 =  11392.92

#               Since 31.53 < 11361.39 -> Really big significant difference!
